class="tocCollapsible_ETCw theme-doc-toc-mobile tocMobile_ITEo"><button type=button class="clean-btn tocCollapsibleButton_TO0P">On this page</button></div><div class="theme-doc-markdown markdown"><header><h1>Page</h1></header><p>Page provides methods to interact with a single tab in a <a title=Browser class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser>Browser</a>, or an <a href=https://developer.chrome.com/extensions/background_pages target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">extension background page</a> in Chromium. One <a title=Browser class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser>Browser</a> instance might have multiple <a title=Page class="" href=/docs/api/class-page>Page</a> instances.</p>

<p>The Page class emits various events (described below) which can be handled using any of Node's native <a href=https://nodejs.org/api/events.html#events_class_eventemitter target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>EventEmitter</code></a> methods, such as <code>on</code>, <code>once</code> or <code>removeListener</code>.</p>
<p>This example logs a message for a single page <code>load</code> event:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">once</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'load'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token arrow operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=></span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token console class-name" style="color:rgb(78, 201, 176)">console</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">log</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'Page loaded!'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<hr/>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=methods>Methods<a href=#methods class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to Methods" title="Direct link to Methods" translate=no>​</a></h2>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-add-init-script>addInitScript<a href=#page-add-init-script class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to addInitScript" title="Direct link to addInitScript" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.addInitScript</x-search>
<p>Adds a script which would be evaluated in one of the following scenarios:</p>
<ul>
<li class="">Whenever the page is navigated.</li>
<li class="">Whenever the child frame is attached or navigated. In this case, the script is evaluated in the context of the newly attached frame.</li>
</ul>
<p>The script is evaluated after the document was created but before any of its scripts were run. This is useful to amend the JavaScript environment, e.g. to seed <code>Math.random</code>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>An example of overriding <code>Math.random</code> before the page loads:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// preload.js</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token known-class-name class-name" style="color:rgb(78, 201, 176)">Math</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method-variable function-variable method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">random</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token arrow operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=></span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token number" style=color:#B5CEA8>42</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>script</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-init-script-option-script></a><a href=#page-add-init-script-option-script class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>path</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<p>Path to the JavaScript file. If <code>path</code> is a relative path, then it is resolved relative to the current working directory. Optional.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>content</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<p>Raw script content. Optional.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>Script to be evaluated in the page.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>arg</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/JSON/stringify#Description target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Serializable class="">Serializable</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-init-script-option-arg></a><a href=#page-add-init-script-option-arg class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Optional argument to pass to <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-add-init-script-option-script>script</a> (only supported when passing a function).</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>exposeFunctions</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-init-script-option-expose-functions></a><a href=#page-add-init-script-option-expose-functions class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set to <code>true</code>, functions passed inside <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-add-init-script-option-arg>arg</a> are exposed in the page and can be called from the init script. Calling one returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a> of its result. Under the hood, each function is exposed via <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-function>page.exposeFunction()</a>, so it is technically accessible from all frames of the page. Unlike functions passed to <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate>page.evaluate()</a>, functions passed to an init script are exposed in every new document, so they survive navigations. Defaults to <code>false</code>, in which case functions are not serializable and are silently dropped.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a title=Disposable class="" href=/docs/api/class-disposable>Disposable</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-init-script-return></a><a href=#page-add-init-script-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-add-locator-handler>addLocatorHandler<a href=#page-add-locator-handler class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to addLocatorHandler" title="Direct link to addLocatorHandler" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.42</font>
<x-search>page.addLocatorHandler</x-search>
<p>When testing a web page, sometimes unexpected overlays like a "Sign up" dialog appear and block actions you want to automate, e.g. clicking a button. These overlays don't always show up in the same way or at the same time, making them tricky to handle in automated tests.</p>
<p>This method lets you set up a special function, called a handler, that activates when it detects that overlay is visible. The handler's job is to remove the overlay, allowing your test to continue as if the overlay wasn't there.</p>
<p>Things to keep in mind:</p>
<ul>
<li class="">When an overlay is shown predictably, we recommend explicitly waiting for it in your test and dismissing it as a part of your normal test flow, instead of using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-add-locator-handler>page.addLocatorHandler()</a>.</li>
<li class="">Playwright checks for the overlay every time before executing or retrying an action that requires an <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability check</a>, or before performing an auto-waiting assertion check. When overlay is visible, Playwright calls the handler first, and then proceeds with the action/assertion. Note that the handler is only called when you perform an action/assertion - if the overlay becomes visible but you don't perform any actions, the handler will not be triggered.</li>
<li class="">After executing the handler, Playwright will ensure that overlay that triggered the handler is not visible anymore. You can opt-out of this behavior with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-add-locator-handler-option-no-wait-after>noWaitAfter</a>.</li>
<li class="">The execution time of the handler counts towards the timeout of the action/assertion that executed the handler. If your handler takes too long, it might cause timeouts.</li>
<li class="">You can register multiple handlers. However, only a single handler will be running at a time. Make sure the actions within a handler don't depend on another handler.</li>
</ul>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>warning</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Running the handler will alter your page state mid-test. For example it will change the currently focused element and move the mouse. Make sure that actions that run after the handler are self-contained and do not rely on the focus and mouse state being unchanged.<p>For example, consider a test that calls <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-focus>locator.focus()</a> followed by <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-keyboard#keyboard-press>keyboard.press()</a>. If your handler clicks a button between these two actions, the focused element most likely will be wrong, and key press will happen on the unexpected element. Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-press>locator.press()</a> instead to avoid this problem.<p>Another example is a series of mouse actions, where <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-mouse#mouse-move>mouse.move()</a> is followed by <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-mouse#mouse-down>mouse.down()</a>. Again, when the handler runs between these two actions, the mouse position will be wrong during the mouse down. Prefer self-contained actions like <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-click>locator.click()</a> that do not rely on the state being unchanged by a handler.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

<p>An example with a custom callback on every actionability check. It uses a <code>&lt;body></code> locator that is always visible, so the handler is called before every actionability check. It is important to specify <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-add-locator-handler-option-no-wait-after>noWaitAfter</a>, because the handler does not hide the <code>&lt;body></code> element.</p>

<p>Handler takes the original locator as an argument. You can also automatically remove the handler after a number of invocations by setting <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-add-locator-handler-option-times>times</a>:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">{</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">  </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> locator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>locator</code> <a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-locator-handler-option-locator></a><a href=#page-add-locator-handler-option-locator class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Locator that triggers the handler.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>handler</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-locator-handler-option-handler></a><a href=#page-add-locator-handler-option-handler class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Function that should be run once <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-add-locator-handler-option-locator>locator</a> appears. This function should get rid of the element that blocks actions like click.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.44</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-locator-handler-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-add-locator-handler-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>By default, after calling the handler Playwright will wait until the overlay becomes hidden, and only then Playwright will continue with the action/assertion that triggered the handler. This option allows to opt-out of this behavior, so that overlay can stay visible after the handler has run.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>times</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.44</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-locator-handler-option-times></a><a href=#page-add-locator-handler-option-times class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Specifies the maximum number of times this handler should be called. Unlimited by default.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-locator-handler-return></a><a href=#page-add-locator-handler-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-add-script-tag>addScriptTag<a href=#page-add-script-tag class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to addScriptTag" title="Direct link to addScriptTag" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.addScriptTag</x-search>
<p>Adds a <code>&lt;script></code> tag into the page with the desired url or content. Returns the added tag when the script's onload fires or when the script content was injected into frame.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">addScriptTag</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">addScriptTag</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>content</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-script-tag-option-content></a><a href=#page-add-script-tag-option-content class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Raw JavaScript content to be injected into frame.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>path</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-script-tag-option-path></a><a href=#page-add-script-tag-option-path class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Path to the JavaScript file to be injected into frame. If <code>path</code> is a relative path, then it is resolved relative to the current working directory.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>type</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-script-tag-option-type></a><a href=#page-add-script-tag-option-type class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Script type. Use 'module' in order to load a JavaScript ES6 module. See <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/script target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">script</a> for more details.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-script-tag-option-url></a><a href=#page-add-script-tag-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>URL of a script to be added.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a title=ElementHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-elementhandle>ElementHandle</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-script-tag-return></a><a href=#page-add-script-tag-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-add-style-tag>addStyleTag<a href=#page-add-style-tag class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to addStyleTag" title="Direct link to addStyleTag" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.addStyleTag</x-search>
<p>Adds a <code>&lt;link rel="stylesheet"></code> tag into the page with the desired url or a <code>&lt;style type="text/css"></code> tag with the content. Returns the added tag when the stylesheet's onload fires or when the CSS content was injected into frame.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">addStyleTag</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">addStyleTag</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>content</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-style-tag-option-content></a><a href=#page-add-style-tag-option-content class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Raw CSS content to be injected into frame.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>path</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-style-tag-option-path></a><a href=#page-add-style-tag-option-path class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Path to the CSS file to be injected into frame. If <code>path</code> is a relative path, then it is resolved relative to the current working directory.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-style-tag-option-url></a><a href=#page-add-style-tag-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>URL of the <code>&lt;link></code> tag.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a title=ElementHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-elementhandle>ElementHandle</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-add-style-tag-return></a><a href=#page-add-style-tag-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-aria-snapshot>ariaSnapshot<a href=#page-aria-snapshot class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to ariaSnapshot" title="Direct link to ariaSnapshot" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.59</font>
<x-search>page.ariaSnapshot</x-search>
<p>Captures the aria snapshot of the page. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/aria-snapshots>aria snapshots</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">ariaSnapshot</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">ariaSnapshot</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>boxes</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.60</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-option-boxes></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-option-boxes class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When <code>true</code>, appends each element's bounding box as <code>[box=x,y,width,height]</code> to the snapshot. Coordinates are relative to the viewport, in CSS pixels, as returned by <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Element/getBoundingClientRect target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>Element.getBoundingClientRect()</code></a>. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>depth</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-option-depth></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-option-depth class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When specified, limits the depth of the snapshot.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>mode</code> "ai" | "default" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-option-mode></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-option-mode class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set to <code>"ai"</code>, returns a snapshot optimized for AI consumption: including element references like <code>[ref=e2]</code> and snapshots of <code>&lt;iframe></code>s. Defaults to <code>"default"</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-option-signal></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-return></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-aria-snapshot-json>ariaSnapshotJSON<a href=#page-aria-snapshot-json class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to ariaSnapshotJSON" title="Direct link to ariaSnapshotJSON" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.63</font>
<x-search>page.ariaSnapshotJSON</x-search>
<p>Captures the aria snapshot of the page as a free form JSON object. Returns the same tree as <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-aria-snapshot>page.ariaSnapshot()</a>, serialized as a JSON value instead of YAML markup. See <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-aria-snapshot-json>locator.ariaSnapshotJSON()</a> for the details of the format.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">ariaSnapshotJSON</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">ariaSnapshotJSON</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>boxes</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-json-option-boxes></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-json-option-boxes class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When <code>true</code>, includes each element's bounding box as a <code>box</code> property with <code>x</code>, <code>y</code>, <code>width</code> and <code>height</code>. Coordinates are relative to the viewport, in CSS pixels, as returned by <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Element/getBoundingClientRect target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>Element.getBoundingClientRect()</code></a>. </li>
<li class="">

<p>When specified, limits the depth of the snapshot.</p>
</li>
<li class="">

<p>When set to <code>"ai"</code>, returns a snapshot optimized for AI consumption: including element references like <code>[ref=e2]</code> and snapshots of <code>&lt;iframe></code>s. Defaults to <code>"default"</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-json-option-signal></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-json-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-aria-snapshot-json-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-aria-snapshot-json-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-close>close<a href=#page-close class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to close" title="Direct link to close" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.close</x-search>
<p>If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-close-option-run-before-unload>runBeforeUnload</a> is <code>false</code>, does not run any unload handlers and waits for the page to be closed. If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-close-option-run-before-unload>runBeforeUnload</a> is <code>true</code> the method will run unload handlers, but will <strong>not</strong> wait for the page to close.</p>
<p>By default, <code>page.close()</code> <strong>does not</strong> run <code>beforeunload</code> handlers.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>if <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-close-option-run-before-unload>runBeforeUnload</a> is passed as true, a <code>beforeunload</code> dialog might be summoned and should be handled manually via <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-event-dialog>page.on('dialog')</a> event.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">close</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">close</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-console-messages>consoleMessages<a href=#page-console-messages class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to consoleMessages" title="Direct link to consoleMessages" translate=no>​</a></h3>

<li class="">
<p><code>includeShadow</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.64</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-content-option-include-shadow></a><a href=#page-content-option-include-shadow class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, contents of open shadow roots are included as <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Web_components/Using_shadow_DOM#declaratively_with_html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">declarative shadow DOM</a>, i.e. <code>&lt;template shadowrootmode="open"></code> elements nested inside their host elements. <h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-drag-and-drop>dragAndDrop<a href=#page-drag-and-drop class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to dragAndDrop" title="Direct link to dragAndDrop" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.13</font>
<x-search>page.dragAndDrop</x-search>
<p>This method drags the source element to the target element. It will first move to the source element, perform a <code>mousedown</code>, then move to the target element and perform a <code>mouseup</code>.</p>

<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>source</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-source></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-source class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element to drag. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>target</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-target></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-target class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element to drop onto. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>force</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-force></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-force class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether to bypass the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option has no effect.</div></div>
<p>This option has no effect.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scroll</code> "auto" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-scroll></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-scroll class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Controls whether Playwright scrolls the element into view before performing the action. Defaults to <code>"auto"</code>, which scrolls the element into view when necessary, including scrolling nested scrollable containers. When set to <code>"none"</code>, Playwright does not scroll the element and the action fails if the element is not already in the viewport. This is useful to assert that an element is reachable by the user without additional scrolling.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-signal></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>sourcePosition</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-source-position></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-source-position class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>x</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>y</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>Clicks on the source element at this point relative to the top-left corner of the element's padding box. If not specified, some visible point of the element is used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>steps</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.57</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-steps></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-steps class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Defaults to 1. Sends <code>n</code> interpolated <code>mousemove</code> events to represent travel between the <code>mousedown</code> and <code>mouseup</code> of the drag. When set to 1, emits a single <code>mousemove</code> event at the destination location.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-strict></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>targetPosition</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-target-position></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-target-position class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>x</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>y</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>Drops on the target element at this point relative to the top-left corner of the element's padding box. If not specified, some visible point of the element is used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>trial</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-option-trial></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-option-trial class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set, this method only performs the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks and skips the action. Defaults to <code>false</code>. Useful to wait until the element is ready for the action without performing it.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-drag-and-drop-return></a><a href=#page-drag-and-drop-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-emulate-media>emulateMedia<a href=#page-emulate-media class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to emulateMedia" title="Direct link to emulateMedia" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.emulateMedia</x-search>
<p>This method changes the <code>CSS media type</code> through the <code>media</code> argument, and/or the <code>'prefers-colors-scheme'</code> media feature, using the <code>colorScheme</code> argument.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

false</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>colorScheme</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | "light" | "dark" | "no-preference" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.9</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-emulate-media-option-color-scheme></a><a href=#page-emulate-media-option-color-scheme class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Emulates <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/@media/prefers-color-scheme target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">prefers-colors-scheme</a> media feature, supported values are <code>'light'</code> and <code>'dark'</code>. Passing <code>null</code> disables color scheme emulation. <code>'no-preference'</code> is deprecated.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>contrast</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | "no-preference" | "more" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.51</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-emulate-media-option-contrast></a><a href=#page-emulate-media-option-contrast class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Emulates <code>'prefers-contrast'</code> media feature, supported values are <code>'no-preference'</code>, <code>'more'</code>. Passing <code>null</code> disables contrast emulation.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>forcedColors</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | "active" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.15</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-emulate-media-option-forced-colors></a><a href=#page-emulate-media-option-forced-colors class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Emulates <code>'forced-colors'</code> media feature, supported values are <code>'active'</code> and <code>'none'</code>. Passing <code>null</code> disables forced colors emulation.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>media</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | "screen" | "print" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.9</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-emulate-media-option-media></a><a href=#page-emulate-media-option-media class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Changes the CSS media type of the page. The only allowed values are <code>'screen'</code>, <code>'print'</code> and <code>null</code>. Passing <code>null</code> disables CSS media emulation.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>reducedMotion</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | "reduce" | "no-preference" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.12</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-emulate-media-option-reduced-motion></a><a href=#page-emulate-media-option-reduced-motion class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Emulates <code>'prefers-reduced-motion'</code> media feature, supported values are <code>'reduce'</code>, <code>'no-preference'</code>. Passing <code>null</code> disables reduced motion emulation.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-emulate-media-return></a><a href=#page-emulate-media-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-evaluate>evaluate<a href=#page-evaluate class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to evaluate" title="Direct link to evaluate" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.evaluate</x-search>
<p>Returns the value of the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-option-expression>pageFunction</a> invocation.</p>
<p>If the function passed to the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate>page.evaluate()</a> returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>, then <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate>page.evaluate()</a> would wait for the promise to resolve and return its value.</p>
<p>If the function passed to the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate>page.evaluate()</a> returns a non-<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/JSON/stringify#Description target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Serializable class="">Serializable</a> value, then <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate>page.evaluate()</a> resolves to <code>undefined</code>. Playwright also supports transferring some additional values that are not serializable by <code>JSON</code>: <code>-0</code>, <code>NaN</code>, <code>Infinity</code>, <code>-Infinity</code>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>Passing argument to <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-option-expression>pageFunction</a>:</p>

<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>pageFunction</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-evaluate-option-expression></a><a href=#page-evaluate-option-expression class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Function to be evaluated in the page context.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>arg</code> <a title=EvaluationArgument class="" href=/docs/evaluating#evaluation-argument>EvaluationArgument</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-evaluate-option-arg></a><a href=#page-evaluate-option-arg class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Optional argument to pass to <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-option-expression>pageFunction</a>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>exposeFunctions</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-evaluate-option-expose-functions></a><a href=#page-evaluate-option-expose-functions class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set to <code>true</code>, functions passed inside <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-option-arg>arg</a> are exposed in the page and can be called from the page function. Calling one returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a> of its result. Under the hood, each function is exposed via <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-function>page.exposeFunction()</a>, so it is technically accessible from all frames of the page. Exposed functions are cleared upon the top-level navigation. Defaults to <code>false</code>, in which case functions are not serializable and passing one throws an error.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/JSON/stringify#Description target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Serializable class="">Serializable</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-evaluate-return></a><a href=#page-evaluate-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-evaluate-handle>evaluateHandle<a href=#page-evaluate-handle class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to evaluateHandle" title="Direct link to evaluateHandle" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.evaluateHandle</x-search>
<p>Returns the value of the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-handle-option-expression>pageFunction</a> invocation as a <a title=JSHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-jshandle>JSHandle</a>.</p>
<p>The only difference between <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate>page.evaluate()</a> and <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-handle>page.evaluateHandle()</a> is that <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-handle>page.evaluateHandle()</a> returns <a title=JSHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-jshandle>JSHandle</a>.</p>
<p>If the function passed to the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-handle>page.evaluateHandle()</a> returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>, then <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-handle>page.evaluateHandle()</a> would wait for the promise to resolve and return its value.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">evaluateHandle</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>A string can also be passed in instead of a function:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> aHandle </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">evaluateHandle</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'document'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// Handle for the 'document'</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><a title=JSHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-jshandle>JSHandle</a> instances can be passed as an argument to the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-handle>page.evaluateHandle()</a>:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token console class-name" style="color:rgb(78, 201, 176)">console</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">log</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> resultHandle</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>pageFunction</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-evaluate-handle-option-expression></a><a href=#page-evaluate-handle-option-expression class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Function to be evaluated in the page context.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>arg</code> <a title=EvaluationArgument class="" href=/docs/evaluating#evaluation-argument>EvaluationArgument</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-evaluate-handle-option-arg></a><a href=#page-evaluate-handle-option-arg class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Optional argument to pass to <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-handle-option-expression>pageFunction</a>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>exposeFunctions</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-evaluate-handle-option-expose-functions></a><a href=#page-evaluate-handle-option-expose-functions class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set to <code>true</code>, functions passed inside <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-evaluate-handle-option-arg>arg</a> are exposed in the page and can be called from the page function. Calling one returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a> of its result. Under the hood, each function is exposed via <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-function>page.exposeFunction()</a>, so it is technically accessible from all frames of the page. Exposed functions are cleared upon the top-level navigation. Defaults to <code>false</code>, in which case functions are not serializable and passing one throws an error.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a title=JSHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-jshandle>JSHandle</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-evaluate-handle-return></a><a href=#page-evaluate-handle-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-expose-binding>exposeBinding<a href=#page-expose-binding class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to exposeBinding" title="Direct link to exposeBinding" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.exposeBinding</x-search>
<p>The method adds a function called <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-binding-option-name>name</a> on the <code>window</code> object of every frame in this page. When called, the function executes <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-binding-option-callback>callback</a> and returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a> which resolves to the return value of <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-binding-option-callback>callback</a>. If the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-binding-option-callback>callback</a> returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>, it will be awaited.</p>
<p>The first argument of the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-binding-option-callback>callback</a> function contains information about the caller: <code>{ browserContext: BrowserContext, page: Page, frame: Frame }</code>.</p>
<p>See <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-expose-binding>browserContext.exposeBinding()</a> for the context-wide version.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Functions installed via <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-binding>page.exposeBinding()</a> survive navigations.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-expose-function>exposeFunction<a href=#page-expose-function class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to exposeFunction" title="Direct link to exposeFunction" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.exposeFunction</x-search>
<p>The method adds a function called <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-function-option-name>name</a> on the <code>window</code> object of every frame in the page. When called, the function executes <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-function-option-callback>callback</a> and returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a> which resolves to the return value of <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-function-option-callback>callback</a>.</p>
<p>If the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-function-option-callback>callback</a> returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>, it will be awaited.</p>
<p>See <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-expose-function>browserContext.exposeFunction()</a> for context-wide exposed function.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Functions installed via <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-expose-function>page.exposeFunction()</a> survive navigations.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>An example of adding a <code>sha256</code> function to the page:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">{</span><span class="token plain"> webkit </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">}</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">require</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'playwright'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain">
