<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-frame>frame<a href=#page-frame class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to frame" title="Direct link to frame" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.frame</x-search>
<p>Returns frame matching the specified criteria. Either <code>name</code> or <code>url</code> must be specified.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> frame </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">frame</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'frame-name'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-frame-locator>frameLocator<a href=#page-frame-locator class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to frameLocator" title="Direct link to frameLocator" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.17</font>
<x-search>page.frameLocator</x-search>
<p>When working with iframes, you can create a frame locator that will enter the iframe and allow selecting elements in that iframe.</p>
<p>When called without <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-frame-locator-option-selector>selector</a>, the search starts in any frame on the page - the main frame or any of the iframes - so that you don't need to locate each iframe first. Note that the rest of the locator is resolved inside a single frame, just like any other locator. If it matches elements inside multiple frames, an error is thrown.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>Following snippet locates element with text "Submit" in the iframe with id <code>my-frame</code>, like <code>&lt;iframe id="my-frame"></code>:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> locator </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">frameLocator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'#my-iframe'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">getByText</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'Submit'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> locator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>Following snippet locates a button, either in the main frame or in one of the iframes:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> locator </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">frameLocator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">getByRole</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'button'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> locator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-frame-locator-option-selector></a><a href=#page-frame-locator-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector that matches the frame element. When not specified, locator is matched in any frame on the page.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>

</ul>

<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.frames</x-search>

<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">frames</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Array target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Array class="">Array</a>&lt;<a title=Frame class="" href=/docs/api/class-frame>Frame</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-frames-return></a><a href=#page-frames-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-get-by-alt-text>getByAltText<a href=#page-get-by-alt-text class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to getByAltText" title="Direct link to getByAltText" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.27</font>
<x-search>page.getByAltText</x-search>
<p>Allows locating elements by their alt text.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>For example, this method will find the image by alt text "Playwright logo":</p>

<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">getByAltText</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'Playwright logo'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-get-by-label>getByLabel<a href=#page-get-by-label class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to getByLabel" title="Direct link to getByLabel" translate=no>​</a></h3>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-get-by-placeholder>getByPlaceholder<a href=#page-get-by-placeholder class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to getByPlaceholder" title="Direct link to getByPlaceholder" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.27</font>
<x-search>page.getByPlaceholder</x-search>
<p>Allows locating input elements by the placeholder text.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>For example, consider the following DOM structure.</p>
<div class="language-html codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-html codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>input</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6> </span><span class="token tag attr-name" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">type</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation attr-equals" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">"</span><span class="token tag attr-value" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">email</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">"</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6> </span><span class="token tag attr-name" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">placeholder</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation attr-equals" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">"</span><span class="token tag attr-value" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">name@example.com</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">"</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6> </span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">/></span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>You can fill the input after locating it by the placeholder text:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">    </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">getByPlaceholder</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'name@example.com'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">    </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">fill</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'playwright@microsoft.com'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>text</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-placeholder-option-text></a><a href=#page-get-by-placeholder-option-text class=list-anchor>#</a></p>

<li class="">

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-get-by-ref>getByRef<a href=#page-get-by-ref class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to getByRef" title="Direct link to getByRef" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.64</font>
<x-search>page.getByRef</x-search>
<p>Locate element by its aria ref. Refs like <code>[ref=e2]</code> are reported by <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-aria-snapshot>page.ariaSnapshot()</a> when called with the <code>"ai"</code> mode, and resolve against the latest snapshot taken in the element's frame.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>Consider the following aria snapshot.</p>
<div class="language-yaml codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-yaml codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">-</span><span class="token plain"> button "Submit" </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">[</span><span class="token plain">ref=e2</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">]</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>You can locate the button by its ref:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">getByRef</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'e2'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>ref</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-ref-option-ref></a><a href=#page-get-by-ref-option-ref class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Aria ref of the element, for example <code>e2</code> or <code>f1e3</code>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-ref-return></a><a href=#page-get-by-ref-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-get-by-role>getByRole<a href=#page-get-by-role class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to getByRole" title="Direct link to getByRole" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.27</font>
<x-search>page.getByRole</x-search>
<p>Allows locating elements by their <a href=https://www.w3.org/TR/wai-aria-1.2/#roles target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">ARIA role</a>, <a href=https://www.w3.org/TR/wai-aria-1.2/#aria-attributes target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">ARIA attributes</a> and <a href=https://w3c.github.io/accname/#dfn-accessible-name target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">accessible name</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>Consider the following DOM structure.</p>
<div class="language-html codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-html codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token tag punctuation" plain">Sign up</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;/</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>h3</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>label</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">  </span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;</span><span class="token <p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-role-return></a><a href=#page-get-by-role-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Details</strong></p>
<p>Role selector <strong>does not replace</strong> accessibility audits and conformance tests, but rather gives early feedback about the ARIA guidelines.</p>
<p>Many html elements have an implicitly <a href=https://w3c.github.io/html-aam/#html-element-role-mappings target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">defined role</a> that is recognized by the role selector. You can find all the <a href=https://www.w3.org/TR/wai-aria-1.2/#role_definitions target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">supported roles here</a>. ARIA guidelines <strong>do not recommend</strong> duplicating implicit roles and attributes by setting <code>role</code> and/or <code>aria-*</code> attributes to default values.</p>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-get-by-test-id>getByTestId<a href=#page-get-by-test-id class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to getByTestId" title="Direct link to getByTestId" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.27</font>
<x-search>page.getByTestId</x-search>
<p>Locate element by the test id.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>Consider the following DOM structure.</p>
<div class="language-html codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-html codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>button</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6> </span><span class="token tag attr-name" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">data-testid</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation attr-equals" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">"</span><span class="token tag attr-value" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">directions</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">"</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token plain">Itinéraire</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;/</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>button</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>You can locate the element by its test id:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">getByTestId</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'directions'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>testId</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-test-id-option-test-id></a><a href=#page-get-by-test-id-option-test-id class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Id to locate the element by.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-test-id-return></a><a href=#page-get-by-test-id-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Details</strong></p>
<p>By default, the <code>data-testid</code> attribute is used as a test id. Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-selectors#selectors-set-test-id-attribute>selectors.setTestIdAttribute()</a> to configure a different test id attribute if necessary.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-get-by-text>getByText<a href=#page-get-by-text class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to getByText" title="Direct link to getByText" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.27</font>
<x-search>page.getByText</x-search>
<p>Allows locating elements that contain given text.</p>
<p>See also <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-filter>locator.filter()</a> that allows to match by another criteria, like an accessible role, and then filter by the text content.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>Consider the following DOM structure:</p>
<div class="language-html codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-html codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>div</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token plain">Hello </span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>span</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token plain">world</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;/</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>span</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;/</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>div</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>div</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token plain">Hello</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;/</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>div</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>You can locate by text substring, exact string, or a regular expression:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token comment" &lt;div></span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>text</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-text-option-text></a><a href=#page-get-by-text-option-text class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Text to locate the element for.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>exact</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-text-option-exact></a><a href=#page-get-by-text-option-exact class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether to find an exact match: case-sensitive and whole-string. Default to false. Ignored when locating by a regular expression. Note that exact match still trims whitespace.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-text-return></a><a href=#page-get-by-text-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Details</strong></p>
<p>Matching by text always normalizes whitespace, even with exact match. For example, it turns multiple spaces into one, turns line breaks into spaces and ignores leading and trailing whitespace.</p>
<p>Input elements of the type <code>button</code> and <code>submit</code> are matched by their <code>value</code> instead of the text content. For example, locating by text <code>"Log in"</code> matches <code>&lt;input type=button value="Log in"></code>.</p>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-get-by-title>getByTitle<a href=#page-get-by-title class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to getByTitle" title="Direct link to getByTitle" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.27</font>
<x-search>page.getByTitle</x-search>
<p>Allows locating elements by their title attribute.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>Consider the following DOM structure.</p>
<div class="language-html codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-html codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>span</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6> </span><span class="token tag attr-name" style="color:rgb(156, 220, 254)">title</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation attr-equals" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">'</span><span class="token tag attr-value" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">Issues count</span><span class="token tag attr-value punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">'</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><span class="token plain">25 issues</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">&lt;/</span><span class="token tag" style=color:#569CD6>span</span><span class="token tag punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">></span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>You can check the issues count after locating it by the title text:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">expect</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">getByTitle</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'Issues count'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">toHaveText</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'25 issues'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>text</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-title-option-text></a><a href=#page-get-by-title-option-text class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Text to locate the element for.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>exact</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-get-by-title-option-exact></a><a href=#page-get-by-title-option-exact class=list-anchor>#</a></p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-go-back>goBack<a href=#page-go-back class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to goBack" title="Direct link to goBack" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.goBack</x-search>
<p>Returns the main resource response. In case of multiple redirects, the navigation will resolve with the response of the last redirect. If cannot go back, returns <code>null</code>.</p>
<p>Navigate to the previous page in history.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>warning</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><strong>Testing Back/Forward Cache (BFCache) is not supported.</strong> By default, Playwright disables the Back/Forward Cache across all browsers. Even if explicitly enabled, Playwright's internal state relies on network-level navigation events. Because BFCache restores unfreeze the DOM without firing these events, using <code>page.goBack()</code> or <code>page.goForward()</code> to trigger a BFCache restore will result in timeouts and a desynchronized <code>Page</code> state.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">goBack</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">goBack</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-go-back-option-signal></a><a href=#page-go-back-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-go-back-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-go-back-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum operation time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>navigationTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>waitUntil</code> "load" | "domcontentloaded" | "networkidle" | "commit" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-go-back-option-wait-until></a><a href=#page-go-back-option-wait-until class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When to consider operation succeeded, defaults to <code>load</code>. Events can be either:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'domcontentloaded'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>DOMContentLoaded</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'load'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>load</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'networkidle'</code> - <strong>DISCOURAGED</strong> consider operation to be finished when there are no network connections for at least <code>500</code> ms. Don't use this method for testing, rely on web assertions to assess readiness instead.</li>
<li class=""><code>'commit'</code> - consider operation to be finished when network response is received and the document started loading.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | <a title=Response class="" href=/docs/api/class-response>Response</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-go-back-return></a><a href=#page-go-back-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-go-forward>goForward<a href=#page-go-forward class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to goForward" title="Direct link to goForward" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.goForward</x-search>
<p>Returns the main resource response. In case of multiple redirects, the navigation will resolve with the response of the last redirect. If cannot go forward, returns <code>null</code>.</p>
<p>Navigate to the next page in history.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>warning</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><strong>Testing Back/Forward Cache (BFCache) is not supported.</strong> By default, Playwright disables the Back/Forward Cache across all browsers. Even if explicitly enabled, Playwright's internal state relies on network-level navigation events. Because BFCache restores unfreeze the DOM without firing these events, using <code>page.goBack()</code> or <code>page.goForward()</code> to trigger a BFCache restore will result in timeouts and a desynchronized <code>Page</code> state.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">goForward</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">goForward</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-go-forward-option-signal></a><a href=#page-go-forward-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-go-forward-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-go-forward-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum operation time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>navigationTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>waitUntil</code> "load" | "domcontentloaded" | "networkidle" | "commit" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-go-forward-option-wait-until></a><a href=#page-go-forward-option-wait-until class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When to consider operation succeeded, defaults to <code>load</code>. Events can be either:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'domcontentloaded'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>DOMContentLoaded</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'load'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>load</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'networkidle'</code> - <strong>DISCOURAGED</strong> consider operation to be finished when there are no network connections for at least <code>500</code> ms. Don't use this method for testing, rely on web assertions to assess readiness instead.</li>
<li class=""><code>'commit'</code> - consider operation to be finished when network response is received and the document started loading.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>

<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-goto>goto<a href=#page-goto class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to goto" title="Direct link to goto" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.goto</x-search>
<p>Returns the main resource response. In case of multiple redirects, the navigation will resolve with the first non-redirect response.</p>
<p>The method will throw an error if:</p>
<ul>
<li class="">there's an SSL error (e.g. in case of self-signed certificates).</li>
<li class="">target URL is invalid.</li>
<li class="">the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-goto-option-timeout>timeout</a> is exceeded during navigation.</li>
<li class="">the remote server does not respond or is unreachable.</li>
<li class="">the main resource failed to load.</li>
</ul>
<p>The method will not throw an error when any valid HTTP status code is returned by the remote server, including 404 "Not Found" and 500 "Internal Server Error".  The status code for such responses can be retrieved by calling <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-response#response-status>response.status()</a>.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>The method either throws an error or returns a main resource response. The only exceptions are navigation to <code>about:blank</code> or navigation to the same URL with a different hash, which would succeed and return <code>null</code>.</div></div>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Headless mode doesn't support navigation to a PDF document. See the <a href="https://bugs.chromium.org/p/chromium/issues/detail?id=761295" target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">upstream issue</a>.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">goto</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">url</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">goto</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">url</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-goto-option-url></a><a href=#page-goto-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>URL to navigate page to. The url should include scheme, e.g. <code>https://</code>. When a <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context-option-base-url>baseURL</a> via the context options was provided and the passed URL is a path, it gets merged via the <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/URL/URL target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>new URL()</code></a> constructor.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>referer</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-goto-option-referer></a><a href=#page-goto-option-referer class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Referer header value. If provided it will take preference over the referer header value set by <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-extra-http-headers>page.setExtraHTTPHeaders()</a>.</p>

<li class="">

<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">

<p>Maximum operation time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>navigationTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>waitUntil</code> "load" | "domcontentloaded" | "networkidle" | "commit" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-goto-option-wait-until></a><a href=#page-goto-option-wait-until class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When to consider operation succeeded, defaults to <code>load</code>. Events can be either:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'domcontentloaded'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>DOMContentLoaded</code> event is fired.</li>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-hide-highlight>hideHighlight<a href=#page-hide-highlight class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to hideHighlight" title="Direct link to hideHighlight" translate=no>​</a></h3>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-locator>locator<a href=#page-locator class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to locator" title="Direct link to locator" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.14</font>
<x-search>page.locator</x-search>
<p>The method returns an element locator that can be used to perform actions on this page / frame. Locator is resolved to the element immediately before performing an action, so a series of actions on the same locator can in fact be performed on different DOM elements. That would happen if the DOM structure between those actions has changed.</p>
<p><a class="" href=/docs/locators>Learn more about locators</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">locator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">locator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-locator-option-selector></a><a href=#page-locator-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to use when resolving DOM element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>has</code> <a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-locator-option-has></a><a href=#page-locator-option-has class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Narrows down the results of the method to those which contain elements matching this relative locator. For example, <code>article</code> that has <code>text=Playwright</code> matches <code>&lt;article>&lt;div>Playwright&lt;/div>&lt;/article></code>.</p>
<p>Inner locator <strong>must be relative</strong> to the outer locator and is queried starting with the outer locator match, not the document root. For example, you can find <code>content</code> that has <code>div</code> in <code>&lt;article>&lt;content>&lt;div>Playwright&lt;/div>&lt;/content>&lt;/article></code>. However, looking for <code>content</code> that has <code>article div</code> will fail, because the inner locator must be relative and should not use any elements outside the <code>content</code>.</p>
<p>Note that outer and inner locators must belong to the same frame. Inner locator must not contain <a title=FrameLocator class="" href=/docs/api/class-framelocator>FrameLocator</a>s.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>hasNot</code> <a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.33</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-locator-option-has-not></a><a href=#page-locator-option-has-not class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Matches elements that do not contain an element that matches an inner locator. Inner locator is queried against the outer one. For example, <code>article</code> that does not have <code>div</code> matches <code>&lt;article>&lt;span>Playwright&lt;/span>&lt;/article></code>.</p>
<p>Note that outer and inner locators must belong to the same frame. Inner locator must not contain <a title=FrameLocator class="" href=/docs/api/class-framelocator>FrameLocator</a>s.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>hasNotText</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.33</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-locator-option-has-not-text></a><a href=#page-locator-option-has-not-text class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Matches elements that do not contain specified text somewhere inside, possibly in a child or a descendant element. When passed a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a>, matching is case-insensitive and searches for a substring.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>hasText</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-locator-option-has-text></a><a href=#page-locator-option-has-text class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Matches elements containing specified text somewhere inside, possibly in a child or a descendant element. When passed a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a>, matching is case-insensitive and searches for a substring. For example, <code>"Playwright"</code> matches <code>&lt;article>&lt;div>Playwright&lt;/div>&lt;/article></code>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-locator-return></a><a href=#page-locator-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-main-frame>mainFrame<a href=#page-main-frame class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to mainFrame" title="Direct link to mainFrame" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.mainFrame</x-search>
<p>The page's main frame. Page is guaranteed to have a main frame which persists during navigations.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-opener>opener<a href=#page-opener class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to opener" title="Direct link to opener" translate=no>​</a></h3>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-pause>pause<a href=#page-pause class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to pause" title="Direct link to pause" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.pause</x-search>
<p>Pauses script execution. Playwright will stop executing the script and wait for the user to either press the 'Resume' button in the page overlay or to call <code>playwright.resume()</code> in the DevTools console.</p>
<p>User can inspect selectors or perform manual steps while paused. Resume will continue running the original script from the place it was paused.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This method requires Playwright to be started in a headed mode, with a falsy <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsertype#browser-type-launch-option-headless>headless</a> option.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">pause</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pause-return></a><a href=#page-pause-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-pdf>pdf<a href=#page-pdf class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to pdf" title="Direct link to pdf" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.pdf</x-search>
<p>Returns the PDF buffer.</p>
<p><code>page.pdf()</code> generates a pdf of the page with <code>print</code> css media. To generate a pdf with <code>screen</code> media, call <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-emulate-media>page.emulateMedia()</a> before calling <code>page.pdf()</code>:</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>By default, <code>page.pdf()</code> generates a pdf with modified colors for printing. Use the <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/-webkit-print-color-adjust target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>-webkit-print-color-adjust</code></a> property to force rendering of exact colors.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// Generates a PDF with 'screen' media type.</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">emulateMedia</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">{</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token literal-property property" style=color:#9CDCFE>media</span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">:</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'screen'</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">}</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">pdf</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">{</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token literal-property property" style=color:#9CDCFE>path</span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">:</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'page.pdf'</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">}</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>The <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-width>width</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-height>height</a>, and <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-margin>margin</a> options accept values labeled with units. Unlabeled values are treated as pixels.</p>
<p>A few examples:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>page.pdf({width: 100})</code> - prints with width set to 100 pixels</li>
<li class=""><code>page.pdf({width: '100px'})</code> - prints with width set to 100 pixels</li>
<li class=""><code>page.pdf({width: '10cm'})</code> - prints with width set to 10 centimeters.</li>
</ul>
<p>All possible units are:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>px</code> - pixel</li>
<li class=""><code>in</code> - inch</li>
<li class=""><code>cm</code> - centimeter</li>
<li class=""><code>mm</code> - millimeter</li>
</ul>
<p>The <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-format>format</a> options are:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>Letter</code>: 8.5in x 11in</li>
<li class=""><code>Legal</code>: 8.5in x 14in</li>
<li class=""><code>Tabloid</code>: 11in x 17in</li>
<li class=""><code>Ledger</code>: 17in x 11in</li>
<li class=""><code>A0</code>: 33.1in x 46.8in</li>
<li class=""><code>A1</code>: 23.4in x 33.1in</li>
<li class=""><code>A2</code>: 16.54in x 23.4in</li>
<li class=""><code>A3</code>: 11.7in x 16.54in</li>
<li class=""><code>A4</code>: 8.27in x 11.7in</li>
<li class=""><code>A5</code>: 5.83in x 8.27in</li>
<li class=""><code>A6</code>: 4.13in x 5.83in</li>
</ul>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-header-template>headerTemplate</a> and <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-footer-template>footerTemplate</a> markup have the following limitations: > 1. Script tags inside templates are not evaluated. > 2. Page styles are not visible inside templates.</div></div>

<ul>
<li class="">

<li class="">

<p>HTML template for the print footer. Should use the same format as the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-header-template>headerTemplate</a>.</p>

<li class="">
<p><code>format</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pdf-option-format></a><a href=#page-pdf-option-format class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Paper format. If set, takes priority over <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-width>width</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-height>height</a> options. Defaults to 'Letter'.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>headerTemplate</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pdf-option-header-template></a><a href=#page-pdf-option-header-template class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>HTML template for the print header. Should be valid HTML markup with following classes used to inject printing values into them:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'date'</code> formatted print date</li>
<li class=""><code>'title'</code> document title</li>
<li class=""><code>'url'</code> document location</li>
<li class=""><code>'pageNumber'</code> current page number</li>
<li class=""><code>'totalPages'</code> total pages in the document</li>
</ul>

<li class="">

<p>Paper height, accepts values labeled with units.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>landscape</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pdf-option-landscape></a><a href=#page-pdf-option-landscape class=list-anchor>#</a></p>

<li class="">

<li class="">

<p>Bottom margin, accepts values labeled with units. Defaults to <code>0</code>.</p>

<p>Whether or not to embed the document outline into the PDF. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>

<li class="">
