<p>Paper ranges to print, e.g., '1-5, 8, 11-13'. Defaults to the empty string, which means print all pages.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>path</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pdf-option-path></a><a href=#page-pdf-option-path class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>The file path to save the PDF to. If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-path>path</a> is a relative path, then it is resolved relative to the current working directory. If no path is provided, the PDF won't be saved to the disk.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>preferCSSPageSize</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pdf-option-prefer-css-page-size></a><a href=#page-pdf-option-prefer-css-page-size class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Give any CSS <code>@page</code> size declared in the page priority over what is declared in <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-width>width</a> and <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-height>height</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-pdf-option-format>format</a> options. Defaults to <code>false</code>, which will scale the content to fit the paper size.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>printBackground</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pdf-option-print-background></a><a href=#page-pdf-option-print-background class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Print background graphics. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scale</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pdf-option-scale></a><a href=#page-pdf-option-scale class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Scale of the webpage rendering. Defaults to <code>1</code>. Scale amount must be between 0.1 and 2.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>tagged</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.42</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pdf-option-tagged></a><a href=#page-pdf-option-tagged class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether or not to generate tagged (accessible) PDF. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">

<p>Paper width, accepts values labeled with units.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-pick-locator>pickLocator<a href=#page-pick-locator class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to pickLocator" title="Direct link to pickLocator" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.59</font>
<x-search>page.pickLocator</x-search>
<p>Enters pick locator mode where hovering over page elements highlights them and shows the corresponding locator. Once the user clicks an element, the mode is deactivated and the <a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a> for the picked element is returned.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> locator </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">pickLocator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token console class-name" style="color:rgb(78, 201, 176)">console</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">log</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">locator</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-pick-locator-return></a><a href=#page-pick-locator-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-reload>reload<a href=#page-reload class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to reload" title="Direct link to reload" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.reload</x-search>
<p>This method reloads the current page, in the same way as if the user had triggered a browser refresh. Returns the main resource response. In case of multiple redirects, the navigation will resolve with the response of the last redirect.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">reload</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">reload</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-reload-option-signal></a><a href=#page-reload-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-reload-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-reload-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum operation time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>navigationTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>waitUntil</code> "load" | "domcontentloaded" | "networkidle" | "commit" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-reload-option-wait-until></a><a href=#page-reload-option-wait-until class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When to consider operation succeeded, defaults to <code>load</code>. Events can be either:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'domcontentloaded'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>DOMContentLoaded</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'load'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>load</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'networkidle'</code> - <strong>DISCOURAGED</strong> consider operation to be finished when there are no network connections for at least <code>500</code> ms. Don't use this method for testing, rely on web assertions to assess readiness instead.</li>
<li class=""><code>'commit'</code> - consider operation to be finished when network response is received and the document started loading.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | <a title=Response class="" href=/docs/api/class-response>Response</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-reload-return></a><a href=#page-reload-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-remove-all-listeners>removeAllListeners<a href=#page-remove-all-listeners class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to removeAllListeners" title="Direct link to removeAllListeners" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.47</font>
<x-search>page.removeAllListeners</x-search>
<p>Removes all the listeners of the given type (or all registered listeners if no type given). Allows to wait for async listeners to complete or to ignore subsequent errors from these listeners.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> request</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">response</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" <p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>type</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-remove-all-listeners-option-type></a><a href=#page-remove-all-listeners-option-type class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>behavior</code> "wait" | "ignoreErrors" | "default" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-remove-all-listeners-option-behavior></a><a href=#page-remove-all-listeners-option-behavior class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Specifies whether to wait for already running listeners and what to do if they throw errors:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'default'</code> - do not wait for current listener calls (if any) to finish, if the listener throws, it may result in unhandled error</li>
<li class=""><code>'wait'</code> - wait for current listener calls (if any) to finish</li>
<li class=""><code>'ignoreErrors'</code> - do not wait for current listener calls (if any) to finish, all errors thrown by the listeners after removal are silently caught</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-remove-all-listeners-return></a><a href=#page-remove-all-listeners-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-remove-locator-handler>removeLocatorHandler<a href=#page-remove-locator-handler class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to removeLocatorHandler" title="Direct link to removeLocatorHandler" translate=no>​</a></h3>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-request-gc>requestGC<a href=#page-request-gc class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to requestGC" title="Direct link to requestGC" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.48</font>
<x-search>page.requestGC</x-search>
<p>Request the page to perform garbage collection. Note that there is no guarantee that all unreachable objects will be collected.</p>
<p>This is useful to help detect memory leaks. For example, if your page has a large object <code>'suspect'</code> that might be leaked, you can check that it does not leak by using a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/WeakRef target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>WeakRef</code></a>.</p>

In your page, save a WeakRef for the "suspect".</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">evaluate</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token arrow operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=></span><span class="token plain"> globalThis</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token property-access">suspectWeakRef</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>new</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token class-name" style="color:rgb(78, 201, 176)">WeakRef</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">suspect</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// 2. Request garbage collection.</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">requestGC</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// 3. Check that weak ref does not deref to the original object.</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token function" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">expect</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">evaluate</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span <p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-requests>requests<a href=#page-requests class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to requests" title="Direct link to requests" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.56</font>
<x-search>page.requests</x-search>
<p>Returns up to (currently) 100 last network request from this page. See <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-event-request>page.on('request')</a> for more details.</p>
<p>Returned requests should be accessed immediately, otherwise they might be collected to prevent unbounded memory growth as new requests come in. Once collected, retrieving most information about the request is impossible.</p>
<p>Note that requests reported through the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-event-request>page.on('request')</a> request are not collected, so there is a trade off between efficient memory usage with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-requests>page.requests()</a> and the amount of available information reported through <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-event-request>page.on('request')</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">requests</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Array target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Array class="">Array</a>&lt;<a title=Request class="" href=/docs/api/class-request>Request</a>>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-requests-return></a><a href=#page-requests-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-route>route<a href=#page-route class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to route" title="Direct link to route" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.route</x-search>
<p>Routing provides the capability to modify network requests that are made by a page.</p>
<p>Once routing is enabled, every request matching the url pattern will stall unless it's continued, fulfilled or aborted.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>The handler will only be called for the first url if the response is a redirect.</div></div>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-route>page.route()</a> will not intercept requests intercepted by Service Worker. See <a href=https://github.com/microsoft/playwright/issues/1090 target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">this</a> issue. We recommend disabling Service Workers when using request interception by setting <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context-option-service-workers>serviceWorkers</a> to <code>'block'</code>.</div></div>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-route>page.route()</a> will not intercept the first request of a popup page. Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-route>browserContext.route()</a> instead.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>An example of a naive handler that aborts all image requests:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code <p>It is possible to examine the request to decide the route action. For example, mocking all requests that contain some post data, and leaving all other requests as is:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">request</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">postData</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> route</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">fulfill</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">{</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token literal-property property" style=color:#9CDCFE>body</span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">:</span><span class="token 212)">}</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">  </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>else</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">    </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> route</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>continue</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">}</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>If a request matches multiple registered routes, the most recently registered route takes precedence.</p>
<p>Page routes take precedence over browser context routes (set up with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-route>browserContext.route()</a>) when request matches both handlers.</p>
<p>To remove a route with its handler you can use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-unroute>page.unroute()</a>.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Enabling routing disables http cache.</div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/URLPattern target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URLPattern class="">URLPattern</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-option-url></a><a href=#page-route-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A glob pattern, regex pattern, URL pattern, or predicate that receives a <a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a> to match during routing. If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context-option-base-url>baseURL</a> is set in the context options and the provided URL is a string that does not start with <code>*</code>, it is resolved using the <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/URL/URL target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>new URL()</code></a> constructor.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>handler</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a title=Route class="" href=/docs/api/class-route>Route</a>, <a title=Request class="" href=/docs/api/class-request>Request</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a>> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-option-handler></a><a href=#page-route-option-handler class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>handler function to route the request.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>times</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.15</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-option-times></a><a href=#page-route-option-times class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>How often a route should be used. By default it will be used every time.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>

<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-route-from-har>routeFromHAR<a href=#page-route-from-har class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to routeFromHAR" title="Direct link to routeFromHAR" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.23</font>
<x-search>page.routeFromHAR</x-search>
<p>If specified the network requests that are made in the page will be served from the HAR file. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/mock#replaying-from-har>Replaying from HAR</a>.</p>
<p>Playwright will not serve requests intercepted by Service Worker from the HAR file. See <a href=https://github.com/microsoft/playwright/issues/1090 target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">this</a> issue. We recommend disabling Service Workers when using request interception by setting <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context-option-service-workers>serviceWorkers</a> to <code>'block'</code>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">routeFromHAR</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">har</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">routeFromHAR</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">har</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>har</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-from-har-option-har></a><a href=#page-route-from-har-option-har class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Path to a <a href=http://www.softwareishard.com/blog/har-12-spec target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">HAR</a> file with prerecorded network data. If <code>path</code> is a relative path, then it is resolved relative to the current working directory.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>notFound</code> "abort" | "fallback" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-from-har-option-not-found></a><a href=#page-route-from-har-option-not-found class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">If set to 'abort' any request not found in the HAR file will be aborted.</li>
<li class="">If set to 'fallback' missing requests will be sent to the network.</li>
</ul>
<p>Defaults to abort.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>update</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-from-har-option-update></a><a href=#page-route-from-har-option-update class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>If specified, updates the given HAR with the actual network information instead of serving from file. The file is written to disk when <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-close>browserContext.close()</a> is called.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>updateContent</code> "embed" | "attach" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.32</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-from-har-option-update-content></a><a href=#page-route-from-har-option-update-content class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Optional setting to control resource content management. If <code>attach</code> is specified, resources are persisted as separate files or entries in the ZIP archive. If <code>embed</code> is specified, content is stored inline the HAR file.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>updateMode</code> "full" | "minimal" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.32</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-from-har-option-update-mode></a><a href=#page-route-from-har-option-update-mode class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set to <code>minimal</code>, only record information necessary for routing from HAR. This omits sizes, timing, page, cookies, security and other types of HAR information that are not used when replaying from HAR. Defaults to <code>minimal</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-from-har-option-url></a><a href=#page-route-from-har-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A glob pattern, regular expression or predicate to match the request URL. Only requests with URL matching the pattern will be served from the HAR file. If not specified, all requests are served from the HAR file.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-from-har-return></a><a href=#page-route-from-har-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-route-web-socket>routeWebSocket<a href=#page-route-web-socket class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to routeWebSocket" title="Direct link to routeWebSocket" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.48</font>
<x-search>page.routeWebSocket</x-search>
<p>This method allows to modify websocket connections that are made by the page.</p>
<p>Note that only <code>WebSocket</code>s created after this method was called will be routed. It is recommended to call this method before navigating the page.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>Below is an example of a simple mock that responds to a single message. See <a title=WebSocketRoute class="" href=/docs/api/class-websocketroute>WebSocketRoute</a> for more details and examples.</p>

<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/URLPattern target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URLPattern class="">URLPattern</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-web-socket-option-url></a><a href=#page-route-web-socket-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Only WebSockets with the url matching this pattern will be routed. A string pattern can be relative to the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context-option-base-url>baseURL</a> context option.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>handler</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a title=WebSocketRoute class="" href=/docs/api/class-websocketroute>WebSocketRoute</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a>> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-web-socket-option-handler></a><a href=#page-route-web-socket-option-handler class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Handler function to route the WebSocket.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-route-web-socket-return></a><a href=#page-route-web-socket-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-screenshot>screenshot<a href=#page-screenshot class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to screenshot" title="Direct link to screenshot" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.screenshot</x-search>
<p>Returns the buffer with the captured screenshot.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">screenshot</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">screenshot</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>animations</code> "disabled" | "allow" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-animations></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-animations class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set to <code>"disabled"</code>, stops CSS animations, CSS transitions and Web Animations. Animations get different treatment depending on their duration:</p>
<ul>
<li class="">finite animations are fast-forwarded to completion, so they'll fire <code>transitionend</code> event.</li>
<li class="">infinite animations are canceled to initial state, and then played over after the screenshot.</li>
</ul>
<p>Defaults to <code>"allow"</code> that leaves animations untouched.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>caret</code> "hide" | "initial" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-caret></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-caret class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set to <code>"hide"</code>, screenshot will hide text caret. When set to <code>"initial"</code>, text caret behavior will not be changed.  Defaults to <code>"hide"</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>clip</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-clip></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-clip class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>x</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
<p>x-coordinate of top-left corner of clip area</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>y</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
<p>y-coordinate of top-left corner of clip area</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>width</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
<p>width of clipping area</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>height</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
<p>height of clipping area</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>An object which specifies clipping of the resulting image.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>fullPage</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-full-page></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-full-page class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, takes a screenshot of the full scrollable page, instead of the currently visible viewport. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>mask</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Array target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Array class="">Array</a>&lt;<a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a>> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-mask></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-mask class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Specify locators that should be masked when the screenshot is taken. Masked elements will be overlaid with a pink box <code>#FF00FF</code> (customized by <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-screenshot-option-mask-color>maskColor</a>) that completely covers its bounding box. The mask is also applied to invisible elements, see <a class="" href=/docs/locators#matching-only-visible-elements>Matching only visible elements</a> to disable that.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>maskColor</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.35</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-mask-color></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-mask-color class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Specify the color of the overlay box for masked elements, in <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/color_value target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">CSS color format</a>. Default color is pink <code>#FF00FF</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>omitBackground</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-omit-background></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-omit-background class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Hides default white background and allows capturing screenshots with transparency. Not applicable to <code>jpeg</code> images. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>path</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-path></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-path class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>The file path to save the image to. The screenshot type will be inferred from file extension. If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-screenshot-option-path>path</a> is a relative path, then it is resolved relative to the current working directory. If no path is provided, the image won't be saved to the disk.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>quality</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-quality></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-quality class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>The quality of the image, between 0-100. Not applicable to <code>png</code> images. For <code>jpeg</code> the default is <code>80</code>. For <code>webp</code>, a quality of <code>100</code> (the default) produces a lossless image, while lower values use lossy compression.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scale</code> "css" | "device" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-scale></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-scale class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set to <code>"css"</code>, screenshot will have a single pixel per each css pixel on the page. For high-dpi devices, this will keep screenshots small. Using <code>"device"</code> option will produce a single pixel per each device pixel, so screenshots of high-dpi devices will be twice as large or even larger.</p>
<p>Defaults to <code>"device"</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-signal></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>style</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.41</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-style></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-style class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Text of the stylesheet to apply while making the screenshot. This is where you can hide dynamic elements, make elements invisible or change their properties to help you creating repeatable screenshots. This stylesheet pierces the Shadow DOM and applies to the inner frames.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>type</code> "png" | "jpeg" | "webp" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-option-type></a><a href=#page-screenshot-option-type class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Specify screenshot type, defaults to <code>png</code>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://nodejs.org/api/buffer.html#buffer_class_buffer target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Buffer class="">Buffer</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-screenshot-return></a><a href=#page-screenshot-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-set-content>setContent<a href=#page-set-content class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to setContent" title="Direct link to setContent" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.setContent</x-search>
<p>This method internally calls <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Document/write target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">document.write()</a>, inheriting all its specific characteristics and behaviors.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">setContent</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">html</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">setContent</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">html</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>html</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-content-option-html></a><a href=#page-set-content-option-html class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>HTML markup to assign to the page.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-content-option-signal></a><a href=#page-set-content-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-content-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-set-content-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum operation time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>navigationTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>waitUntil</code> "load" | "domcontentloaded" | "networkidle" | "commit" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-content-option-wait-until></a><a href=#page-set-content-option-wait-until class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When to consider operation succeeded, defaults to <code>load</code>. Events can be either:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'domcontentloaded'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>DOMContentLoaded</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'load'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>load</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'networkidle'</code> - <strong>DISCOURAGED</strong> consider operation to be finished when there are no network connections for at least <code>500</code> ms. Don't use this method for testing, rely on web assertions to assess readiness instead.</li>
<li class=""><code>'commit'</code> - consider operation to be finished when network response is received and the document started loading.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-content-return></a><a href=#page-set-content-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-set-default-navigation-timeout>setDefaultNavigationTimeout<a href=#page-set-default-navigation-timeout class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to setDefaultNavigationTimeout" title="Direct link to setDefaultNavigationTimeout" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout</x-search>
<p>This setting will change the default maximum navigation time for the following methods and related shortcuts:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-go-back>page.goBack()</a></li>
<li class=""><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-go-forward>page.goForward()</a></li>
<li class=""><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-goto>page.goto()</a></li>
<li class=""><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-reload>page.reload()</a></li>
<li class=""><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-content>page.setContent()</a></li>
<li class=""><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-navigation>page.waitForNavigation()</a></li>
<li class=""><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-url>page.waitForURL()</a></li>
</ul>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> takes priority over <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> and <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-set-default-timeout>setDefaultTimeout<a href=#page-set-default-timeout class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to setDefaultTimeout" title="Direct link to setDefaultTimeout" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.setDefaultTimeout</x-search>
<p>This setting will change the default maximum time for all the methods accepting <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout-option-timeout>timeout</a> option.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> takes priority over <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">setDefaultTimeout</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">timeout</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<li class="">

<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Pass <code>0</code> to disable timeout.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-set-extra-http-headers>setExtraHTTPHeaders<a href=#page-set-extra-http-headers class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to setExtraHTTPHeaders" title="Direct link to setExtraHTTPHeaders" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.setExtraHTTPHeaders</x-search>
<p>The extra HTTP headers will be sent with every request the page initiates.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-extra-http-headers>page.setExtraHTTPHeaders()</a> does not guarantee the order of headers in the outgoing requests.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">setExtraHTTPHeaders</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">headers</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>headers</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a>, <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-extra-http-headers-option-headers></a><a href=#page-set-extra-http-headers-option-headers class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>An object containing additional HTTP headers to be sent with every request. All header values must be strings.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-extra-http-headers-return></a><a href=#page-set-extra-http-headers-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-set-viewport-size>setViewportSize<a href=#page-set-viewport-size class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to setViewportSize" title="Direct link to setViewportSize" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.setViewportSize</x-search>
<p>In the case of multiple pages in a single browser, each page can have its own viewport size. However, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context>browser.newContext()</a> allows to set viewport size (and more) for all pages in the context at once.</p>
<p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-viewport-size>page.setViewportSize()</a> will resize the page. A lot of websites don't expect phones to change size, so you should set the viewport size before navigating to the page. <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-viewport-size>page.setViewportSize()</a> will also reset <code>screen</code> size, use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context>browser.newContext()</a> with <code>screen</code> and <code>viewport</code> parameters if you need better control of these properties.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code <p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>viewportSize</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-viewport-size-option-viewport-size></a><a href=#page-set-viewport-size-option-viewport-size class=list-anchor>#</a>

<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-title>title<a href=#page-title class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to title" title="Direct link to title" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.title</x-search>
<p>Returns the page's title.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">title</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-title-return></a><a href=#page-title-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
