<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-unroute>unroute<a href=#page-unroute class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to unroute" title="Direct link to unroute" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.unroute</x-search>
<p>Removes a route created with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-route>page.route()</a>. When <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-unroute-option-handler>handler</a> is not specified, removes all routes for the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-unroute-option-url>url</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">unroute</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">url</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">unroute</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">url</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> handler</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/URLPattern target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URLPattern class="">URLPattern</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-unroute-option-url></a><a href=#page-unroute-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A glob pattern, regex pattern, URL pattern, or predicate receiving <a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a> to match while routing.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>handler</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a title=Route class="" href=/docs/api/class-route>Route</a>, <a title=Request class="" href=/docs/api/class-request>Request</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a>> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-unroute-option-handler></a><a href=#page-unroute-option-handler class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Optional handler function to route the request.</p>
</li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-unroute-all>unrouteAll<a href=#page-unroute-all class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to unrouteAll" title="Direct link to unrouteAll" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.41</font>
<x-search>page.unrouteAll</x-search>
<p>Removes all routes created with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-route>page.route()</a> and <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-route-from-har>page.routeFromHAR()</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">unrouteAll</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">unrouteAll</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>behavior</code> "wait" | "ignoreErrors" | "default" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-unroute-all-option-behavior></a><a href=#page-unroute-all-option-behavior class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Specifies whether to wait for already running handlers and what to do if they throw errors:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'default'</code> - do not wait for current handler calls (if any) to finish, if unrouted handler throws, it may result in unhandled error</li>
<li class=""><code>'wait'</code> - wait for current handler calls (if any) to finish</li>
<li class=""><code>'ignoreErrors'</code> - do not wait for current handler calls (if any) to finish, all errors thrown by the handlers after unrouting are silently caught</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-unroute-all-return></a><a href=#page-unroute-all-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-event>waitForEvent<a href=#page-wait-for-event class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForEvent" title="Direct link to waitForEvent" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.waitForEvent</x-search>
<p>Waits for event to fire and passes its value into the predicate function. Returns when the predicate returns truthy value. Will throw an error if the page is closed before the event is fired. Returns the event data value.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// Start waiting for download before clicking. <p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>event</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-event-option-event></a><a href=#page-wait-for-event-option-event class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Event name, same one typically passed into <code>*.on(event)</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>optionsOrPredicate</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-event-option-options-or-predicate></a><a href=#page-wait-for-event-option-options-or-predicate class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>predicate</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a></p>
<p>Receives the event data and resolves to truthy value when the waiting should resolve.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<p>Maximum time to wait for in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>Either a predicate that receives an event or an options object. Optional.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>predicate</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-event-option-predicate></a><a href=#page-wait-for-event-option-predicate class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Receives the event data and resolves to truthy value when the waiting should resolve.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-event-option-signal></a><a href=#page-wait-for-event-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the waiting using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the waiting will be aborted and the operation will throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-event-return></a><a href=#page-wait-for-event-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-function>waitForFunction<a href=#page-wait-for-function class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForFunction" title="Direct link to waitForFunction" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.waitForFunction</x-search>
<p>Returns when the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-function-option-expression>pageFunction</a> returns a truthy value. It resolves to a JSHandle of the truthy value.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>The <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-function>page.waitForFunction()</a> can be used to observe viewport size change:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" <p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>pageFunction</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-function-option-expression></a><a href=#page-wait-for-function-option-expression class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Function to be evaluated in the page context.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>arg</code> <a title=EvaluationArgument class="" href=/docs/evaluating#evaluation-argument>EvaluationArgument</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-function-option-arg></a><a href=#page-wait-for-function-option-arg class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Optional argument to pass to <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-function-option-expression>pageFunction</a>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>polling</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> | "raf" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-function-option-polling></a><a href=#page-wait-for-function-option-polling class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-function-option-polling>polling</a> is <code>'raf'</code>, then <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-function-option-expression>pageFunction</a> is constantly executed in <code>requestAnimationFrame</code> callback. If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-function-option-polling>polling</a> is a number, then it is treated as an interval in milliseconds at which the function would be executed. Defaults to <code>raf</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-function-option-signal></a><a href=#page-wait-for-function-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-function-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-wait-for-function-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time to wait for in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a title=JSHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-jshandle>JSHandle</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-function-return></a><a href=#page-wait-for-function-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-load-state>waitForLoadState<a href=#page-wait-for-load-state class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForLoadState" title="Direct link to waitForLoadState" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.waitForLoadState</x-search>
<p>Returns when the required load state has been reached.</p>
<p>This resolves when the page reaches a required load state, <code>load</code> by default. The navigation must have been committed when this method is called. If current document has already reached the required state, resolves immediately.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Most of the time, this method is not needed because Playwright <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>auto-waits before every action</a>.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>state</code> "load" | "domcontentloaded" | "networkidle" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-load-state-option-state></a><a href=#page-wait-for-load-state-option-state class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Optional load state to wait for, defaults to <code>load</code>. If the state has been already reached while loading current document, the method resolves immediately. Can be one of:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'load'</code> - wait for the <code>load</code> event to be fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'domcontentloaded'</code> - wait for the <code>DOMContentLoaded</code> event to be fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'networkidle'</code> - <strong>DISCOURAGED</strong> wait until there are no network connections for at least <code>500</code> ms. Don't use this method for testing, rely on web assertions to assess readiness instead.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-load-state-option-signal></a><a href=#page-wait-for-load-state-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the waiting using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the waiting will be aborted and the operation will throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-load-state-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-wait-for-load-state-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum operation time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>navigationTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-load-state-return></a><a href=#page-wait-for-load-state-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-request>waitForRequest<a href=#page-wait-for-request class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForRequest" title="Direct link to waitForRequest" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.waitForRequest</x-search>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-response>waitForResponse<a href=#page-wait-for-response class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForResponse" title="Direct link to waitForResponse" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>

<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>urlOrPredicate</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a title=Response class="" href=/docs/api/class-response>Response</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-response-option-url-or-predicate></a><a href=#page-wait-for-response-option-url-or-predicate class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Request URL string, regex or predicate receiving <a title=Response class="" href=/docs/api/class-response>Response</a> object. When a <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context-option-base-url>baseURL</a> via the context options was provided and the passed URL is a path, it gets merged via the <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/URL/URL target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>new URL()</code></a> constructor.</p>

<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-response-option-signal></a><a href=#page-wait-for-response-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the waiting using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the waiting will be aborted and the operation will throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-response-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-wait-for-response-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum wait time in milliseconds, defaults to 30 seconds, pass <code>0</code> to disable the timeout. The default value can be changed by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a title=Response class="" href=/docs/api/class-response>Response</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-response-return></a><a href=#page-wait-for-response-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-url>waitForURL<a href=#page-wait-for-url class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForURL" title="Direct link to waitForURL" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.11</font>
<x-search>page.waitForURL</x-search>
<p>Waits for the main frame to navigate to the given URL.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'a.delayed-navigation'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// Clicking the link will indirectly cause a navigation</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">waitForURL</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'**/target.html'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/URLPattern target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URLPattern class="">URLPattern</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-url-option-url></a><a href=#page-wait-for-url-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A glob pattern, regex pattern, URL pattern, or predicate receiving <a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a> to match while waiting for the navigation. Note that if the parameter is a string without wildcard characters, the method will wait for navigation to URL that is exactly equal to the string.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-url-option-signal></a><a href=#page-wait-for-url-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the waiting using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the waiting will be aborted and the operation will throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-url-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-wait-for-url-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum operation time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>navigationTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>waitUntil</code> "load" | "domcontentloaded" | "networkidle" | "commit" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-url-option-wait-until></a><a href=#page-wait-for-url-option-wait-until class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When to consider operation succeeded, defaults to <code>load</code>. Events can be either:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'domcontentloaded'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>DOMContentLoaded</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'load'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>load</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'networkidle'</code> - <strong>DISCOURAGED</strong> consider operation to be finished when there are no network connections for at least <code>500</code> ms. Don't use this method for testing, rely on web assertions to assess readiness instead.</li>
<li class=""><code>'commit'</code> - consider operation to be finished when network response is received and the document started loading.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-url-return></a><a href=#page-wait-for-url-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-console>on('console')<a href=#page-event-console class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('console')" title="Direct link to on('console')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-crash>on('crash')<a href=#page-event-crash class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('crash')" title="Direct link to on('crash')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.on('crash')</x-search>
<p>Emitted when the page crashes. Browser pages might crash if they try to allocate too much memory. When the page crashes, ongoing and subsequent operations will throw.</p>
<p>The most common way to deal with crashes is to catch an exception:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>try</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">{</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">  </span><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// Crash might happen during a click.</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">  </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'button'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"> <h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-dialog>on('dialog')<a href=#page-event-dialog class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('dialog')" title="Direct link to on('dialog')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.on('dialog')</x-search>
<p>Emitted when a JavaScript dialog appears, such as <code>alert</code>, <code>prompt</code>, <code>confirm</code> or <code>beforeunload</code>. Listener <strong>must</strong> either <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-dialog#dialog-accept>dialog.accept()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-dialog#dialog-dismiss>dialog.dismiss()</a> the dialog - otherwise the page will <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/EventLoop#never_blocking target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">freeze</a> waiting for the dialog, and actions like click will never finish.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">on</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'dialog'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token parameter" style=color:#9CDCFE>dialog</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token arrow operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=></span><span class="token plain"> dialog</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">accept</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>When no <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-event-dialog>page.on('dialog')</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-event-dialog>browserContext.on('dialog')</a> listeners are present, all dialogs are automatically dismissed.</div></div>
<p><strong>Event data</strong></p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-dialog-closed>on('dialogclosed')<a href=#page-event-dialog-closed class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('dialogclosed')" title="Direct link to on('dialogclosed')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.63</font>
<x-search>page.on('dialogclosed')</x-search>
<p>Emitted when a JavaScript dialog has been closed, either by <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-dialog#dialog-accept>dialog.accept()</a>, by <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-dialog#dialog-dismiss>dialog.dismiss()</a>, or manually by the user in the headed browser.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-file-chooser>on('filechooser')<a href=#page-event-file-chooser class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('filechooser')" title="Direct link to on('filechooser')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.on('filechooser')</x-search>

Playwright can respond to it via setting the input files using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-filechooser#file-chooser-set-files>fileChooser.setFiles()</a> that can be uploaded after that.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-popup>on('popup')<a href=#page-event-popup class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('popup')" title="Direct link to on('popup')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.on('popup')</x-search>
<p>Emitted when the page opens a new tab or window. This event is emitted in addition to the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-event-page>browserContext.on('page')</a>, but only for popups relevant to this page.</p>
<p>The earliest moment that page is available is when it has navigated to the initial url. For example, when opening a popup with <code>window.open('http://example.com')</code>, this event will fire when the network request to "<a href=http://example.com target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">http://example.com</a>" is done and its response has started loading in the popup. If you would like to route/listen to this network request, use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-route>browserContext.route()</a> and <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-event-request>browserContext.on('request')</a> respectively instead of similar methods on the <a title=Page class="" href=/docs/api/class-page>Page</a>.</p>

<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-load-state>page.waitForLoadState()</a> to wait until the page gets to a particular state (you should not need it in most cases).</div></div>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-request>on('request')<a href=#page-event-request class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('request')" title="Direct link to on('request')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.on('request')</x-search>
<p>Emitted when a page issues a request. The <a title=Request class="" href=/docs/api/class-request>request</a> object is read-only. In order to intercept and mutate requests, see <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-route>page.route()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-route>browserContext.route()</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain">page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">on</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'request'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token parameter" style=color:#9CDCFE>data</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token arrow operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=></span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">{</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">}</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>HTTP Error responses, such as 404 or 503, are still successful responses from HTTP standpoint, so request will complete with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-event-request-finished>page.on('requestfinished')</a> event and not with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-event-request-failed>page.on('requestfailed')</a>. A request will only be considered failed when the client cannot get an HTTP response from the server, e.g. due to network error net::ERR_FAILED.</div></div>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-request-finished>on('requestfinished')<a href=#page-event-request-finished class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('requestfinished')" title="Direct link to on('requestfinished')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.on('requestfinished')</x-search>
<p>Emitted when a request finishes successfully after downloading the response body. For a successful response, the sequence of events is <code>request</code>, <code>response</code> and <code>requestfinished</code>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-event-response>on('response')<a href=#page-event-response class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to on('response')" title="Direct link to on('response')" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.on('response')</x-search>
<p>Emitted when <a title=Response class="" href=/docs/api/class-response>response</a> status and headers are received for a request. For a successful response, the sequence of events is <code>request</code>, <code>response</code> and <code>requestfinished</code>.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-query-selector>$<a href=#page-query-selector class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to $" title="Direct link to $" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.$</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-locator>page.locator()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>The method finds an element matching the specified selector within the page. If no elements match the selector, the return value resolves to <code>null</code>. To wait for an element on the page, use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-wait-for>locator.waitFor()</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">$</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">$</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-query-selector-option-selector></a><a href=#page-query-selector-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to query for.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-query-selector-option-strict></a><a href=#page-query-selector-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | <a title=ElementHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-elementhandle>ElementHandle</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-query-selector-return></a><a href=#page-query-selector-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-query-selector-all>$$<a href=#page-query-selector-all class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to $$" title="Direct link to $$" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.$$</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-locator>page.locator()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>The method finds all elements matching the specified selector within the page. If no elements match the selector, the return value resolves to <code>[]</code>.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-eval-on-selector>$eval<a href=#page-eval-on-selector class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to $eval" title="Direct link to $eval" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.$eval</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This method does not wait for the element to pass actionability checks and therefore can lead to the flaky tests. Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-evaluate>locator.evaluate()</a>, other <a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a> helper methods or web-first assertions instead.</div></div>
<p>The method finds an element matching the specified selector within the page and passes it as a first argument to <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-eval-on-selector-option-expression>pageFunction</a>. If no elements match the selector, the method throws an error. Returns the value of <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-eval-on-selector-option-expression>pageFunction</a>.</p>
<p>If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-eval-on-selector-option-expression>pageFunction</a> returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>, then <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-eval-on-selector>page.$eval()</a> would wait for the promise to resolve and return its value.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> searchValue </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">$eval</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'#search'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token parameter" style=color:#9CDCFE>el</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token arrow operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=></span><span class="token plain"> el</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, <h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-eval-on-selector-all>$$eval<a href=#page-eval-on-selector-all class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to $$eval" title="Direct link to $$eval" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.$$eval</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>In most cases, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-evaluate-all>locator.evaluateAll()</a>, other <a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a> helper methods and web-first assertions do a better job.</div></div>
<p>The method finds all elements matching the specified selector within the page and passes an array of matched elements as a first argument to <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-eval-on-selector-all-option-expression>pageFunction</a>. Returns the result of <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-eval-on-selector-all-option-expression>pageFunction</a> invocation.</p>
<p>If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-eval-on-selector-all-option-expression>pageFunction</a> returns a <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>, then <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-eval-on-selector-all>page.$$eval()</a> would wait for the promise to resolve and return its value.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
