<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-check>check<a href=#page-check class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to check" title="Direct link to check" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.check</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-check>locator.check()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method checks an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-check-option-selector>selector</a> by performing the following steps:</p>
<ol>
<li class="">Find an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-check-option-selector>selector</a>. If there is none, wait until a matching element is attached to the DOM.</li>
<li class="">Ensure that matched element is a checkbox or a radio input. If not, this method throws. If the element is already checked, this method returns immediately.</li>
<li class="">Wait for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks on the matched element, unless <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-check-option-force>force</a> option is set. If the element is detached during the checks, the whole action is retried.</li>
<li class="">Scroll the element into view if needed.</li>
<li class="">Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-mouse>page.mouse</a> to click in the center of the element.</li>
<li class="">Ensure that the element is now checked. If not, this method throws.</li>
</ol>
<p>When all steps combined have not finished during the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-check-option-timeout>timeout</a>, this method throws a <a title=TimeoutError class="" href=/docs/api/class-timeouterror>TimeoutError</a>. Passing zero timeout disables this.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">check</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">check</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-selector></a><a href=#page-check-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>force</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-force></a><a href=#page-check-option-force class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether to bypass the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-check-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option has no effect.</div></div>
<p>This option has no effect.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>position</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.11</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-position></a><a href=#page-check-option-position class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>x</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>y</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>A point to use relative to the top-left corner of element padding box. If not specified, uses some visible point of the element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scroll</code> "auto" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-scroll></a><a href=#page-check-option-scroll class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Controls whether Playwright scrolls the element into view before performing the action. Defaults to <code>"auto"</code>, which scrolls the element into view when necessary, including scrolling nested scrollable containers. When set to <code>"none"</code>, Playwright does not scroll the element and the action fails if the element is not already in the viewport. This is useful to assert that an element is reachable by the user without additional scrolling.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-signal></a><a href=#page-check-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-strict></a><a href=#page-check-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-check-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>trial</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.11</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-option-trial></a><a href=#page-check-option-trial class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set, this method only performs the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks and skips the action. Defaults to <code>false</code>. Useful to wait until the element is ready for the action without performing it.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-check-return></a><a href=#page-check-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-click>click<a href=#page-click class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to click" title="Direct link to click" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.click</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-click>locator.click()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method clicks an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-click-option-selector>selector</a> by performing the following steps:</p>
<ol>
<li class="">Find an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-click-option-selector>selector</a>. If there is none, wait until a matching element is attached to the DOM.</li>
<li class="">Wait for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks on the matched element, unless <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-click-option-force>force</a> option is set. If the element is detached during the checks, the whole action is retried.</li>
<li class="">Scroll the element into view if needed.</li>
<li class="">Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-mouse>page.mouse</a> to click in the center of the element, or the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-click-option-position>position</a>.</li>
<li class="">Wait for initiated navigations to either succeed or fail, unless <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-click-option-no-wait-after>noWaitAfter</a> option is set.</li>
</ol>
<p>When all steps combined have not finished during the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-click-option-timeout>timeout</a>, this method throws a <a title=TimeoutError class="" href=/docs/api/class-timeouterror>TimeoutError</a>. Passing zero timeout disables this.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-selector></a><a href=#page-click-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>button</code> "left" | "right" | "middle" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-button></a><a href=#page-click-option-button class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Defaults to <code>left</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>clickCount</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-click-count></a><a href=#page-click-option-click-count class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>defaults to 1. See <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/UIEvent/detail target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=UIEvent.detail class="">UIEvent.detail</a>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>delay</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-delay></a><a href=#page-click-option-delay class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Time to wait between <code>mousedown</code> and <code>mouseup</code> in milliseconds. Defaults to 0.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>force</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-force></a><a href=#page-click-option-force class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether to bypass the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>modifiers</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Array target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Array class="">Array</a>&lt;"Alt" | "Control" | "ControlOrMeta" | "Meta" | "Shift"> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-modifiers></a><a href=#page-click-option-modifiers class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Modifier keys to press. Ensures that only these modifiers are pressed during the operation, and then restores current modifiers back. If not specified, currently pressed modifiers are used. "ControlOrMeta" resolves to "Control" on Windows and Linux and to "Meta" on macOS.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-click-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option will default to <code>true</code> in the future.</div></div>
<p>Actions that initiate navigations are waiting for these navigations to happen and for pages to start loading. You can opt out of waiting via setting this flag. You would only need this option in the exceptional cases such as navigating to inaccessible pages. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>position</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-position></a><a href=#page-click-option-position class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>x</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>y</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>A point to use relative to the top-left corner of element padding box. If not specified, uses some visible point of the element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scroll</code> "auto" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-scroll></a><a href=#page-click-option-scroll class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Controls whether Playwright scrolls the element into view before performing the action. Defaults to <code>"auto"</code>, which scrolls the element into view when necessary, including scrolling nested scrollable containers. When set to <code>"none"</code>, Playwright does not scroll the element and the action fails if the element is not already in the viewport. This is useful to assert that an element is reachable by the user without additional scrolling.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-signal></a><a href=#page-click-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-strict></a><a href=#page-click-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-click-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>trial</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.11</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-option-trial></a><a href=#page-click-option-trial class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set, this method only performs the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks and skips the action. Defaults to <code>false</code>. Useful to wait until the element is ready for the action without performing it. Note that keyboard <code>modifiers</code> will be pressed regardless of <code>trial</code> to allow testing elements which are only visible when those keys are pressed.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-click-return></a><a href=#page-click-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-dblclick>dblclick<a href=#page-dblclick class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to dblclick" title="Direct link to dblclick" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.dblclick</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-dblclick>locator.dblclick()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method double clicks an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-dblclick-option-selector>selector</a> by performing the following steps:</p>
<ol>
<li class="">Find an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-dblclick-option-selector>selector</a>. If there is none, wait until a matching element is attached to the DOM.</li>
<li class="">Wait for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks on the matched element, unless <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-dblclick-option-force>force</a> option is set. If the element is detached during the checks, the whole action is retried.</li>
<li class="">Scroll the element into view if needed.</li>
<li class="">Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-mouse>page.mouse</a> to double click in the center of the element, or the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-dblclick-option-position>position</a>.</li>
</ol>
<p>When all steps combined have not finished during the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-dblclick-option-timeout>timeout</a>, this method throws a <a title=TimeoutError class="" href=/docs/api/class-timeouterror>TimeoutError</a>. Passing zero timeout disables this.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><code>page.dblclick()</code> dispatches two <code>click</code> events and a single <code>dblclick</code> event.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">dblclick</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">dblclick</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-selector></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>button</code> "left" | "right" | "middle" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-button></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-button class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Defaults to <code>left</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>delay</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-delay></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-delay class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Time to wait between <code>mousedown</code> and <code>mouseup</code> in milliseconds. Defaults to 0.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>force</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-force></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-force class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether to bypass the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>modifiers</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Array target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Array class="">Array</a>&lt;"Alt" | "Control" | "ControlOrMeta" | "Meta" | "Shift"> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-modifiers></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-modifiers class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Modifier keys to press. Ensures that only these modifiers are pressed during the operation, and then restores current modifiers back. If not specified, currently pressed modifiers are used. "ControlOrMeta" resolves to "Control" on Windows and Linux and to "Meta" on macOS.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option has no effect.</div></div>
<p>This option has no effect.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>position</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-position></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-position class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>x</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>y</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>A point to use relative to the top-left corner of element padding box. If not specified, uses some visible point of the element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scroll</code> "auto" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-scroll></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-scroll class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Controls whether Playwright scrolls the element into view before performing the action. Defaults to <code>"auto"</code>, which scrolls the element into view when necessary, including scrolling nested scrollable containers. When set to <code>"none"</code>, Playwright does not scroll the element and the action fails if the element is not already in the viewport. This is useful to assert that an element is reachable by the user without additional scrolling.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-signal></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-strict></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>trial</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.11</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-option-trial></a><a href=#page-dblclick-option-trial class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set, this method only performs the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks and skips the action. Defaults to <code>false</code>. Useful to wait until the element is ready for the action without performing it. Note that keyboard <code>modifiers</code> will be pressed regardless of <code>trial</code> to allow testing elements which are only visible when those keys are pressed.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dblclick-return></a><a href=#page-dblclick-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-dispatch-event>dispatchEvent<a href=#page-dispatch-event class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to dispatchEvent" title="Direct link to dispatchEvent" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.dispatchEvent</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-dispatch-event>locator.dispatchEvent()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>The snippet below dispatches the <code>click</code> event on the element. Regardless of the visibility state of the element, <code>click</code> is dispatched. This is equivalent to calling <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLElement/click target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">element.click()</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">dispatchEvent</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'button#submit'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'click'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p>Under the hood, it creates an instance of an event based on the given <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-dispatch-event-option-type>type</a>, initializes it with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-dispatch-event-option-event-init>eventInit</a> properties and dispatches it on the element. Events are <code>composed</code>, <code>cancelable</code> and bubble by default.</p>
<p>Since <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-dispatch-event-option-event-init>eventInit</a> is event-specific, please refer to the events documentation for the lists of initial properties:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/DeviceMotionEvent/DeviceMotionEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">DeviceMotionEvent</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/DeviceOrientationEvent/DeviceOrientationEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">DeviceOrientationEvent</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/DragEvent/DragEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">DragEvent</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Event/Event target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">Event</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/FocusEvent/FocusEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">FocusEvent</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/KeyboardEvent/KeyboardEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">KeyboardEvent</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/MouseEvent/MouseEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">MouseEvent</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/PointerEvent/PointerEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">PointerEvent</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/TouchEvent/TouchEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">TouchEvent</a></li>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/WheelEvent/WheelEvent target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">WheelEvent</a></li>
</ul>
<p>You can also specify <code>JSHandle</code> as the property value if you want live objects to be passed into the event:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// Note you can only create DataTransfer in Chromium and Firefox</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">dispatchEvent</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'#source'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, <p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dispatch-event-option-selector></a><a href=#page-dispatch-event-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>type</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dispatch-event-option-type></a><a href=#page-dispatch-event-option-type class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>DOM event type: <code>"click"</code>, <code>"dragstart"</code>, etc.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>eventInit</code> <a title=EvaluationArgument class="" href=/docs/evaluating#evaluation-argument>EvaluationArgument</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dispatch-event-option-event-init></a><a href=#page-dispatch-event-option-event-init class=list-anchor>#</a></p>

</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dispatch-event-option-signal></a><a href=#page-dispatch-event-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dispatch-event-option-strict></a><a href=#page-dispatch-event-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-dispatch-event-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-dispatch-event-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-fill>fill<a href=#page-fill class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to fill" title="Direct link to fill" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.fill</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-fill>locator.fill()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method waits for an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-fill-option-selector>selector</a>, waits for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks, focuses the element, fills it and triggers an <code>input</code> event after filling. Note that you can pass an empty string to clear the input field.</p>
<p>If the target element is not an <code>&lt;input></code>, <code>&lt;textarea></code> or <code>[contenteditable]</code> element, this method throws an error. However, if the element is inside the <code>&lt;label></code> element that has an associated <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLLabelElement/control target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">control</a>, the control will be filled instead.</p>
<p>To send fine-grained keyboard events, use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-press-sequentially>locator.pressSequentially()</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">fill</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> value</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">fill</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> value</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-fill-option-selector></a><a href=#page-fill-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>value</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-fill-option-value></a><a href=#page-fill-option-value class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Value to fill for the <code>&lt;input></code>, <code>&lt;textarea></code> or <code>[contenteditable]</code> element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>force</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.13</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-fill-option-force></a><a href=#page-fill-option-force class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether to bypass the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-fill-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-fill-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option has no effect.</div></div>
<p>This option has no effect.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-fill-option-signal></a><a href=#page-fill-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-fill-option-strict></a><a href=#page-fill-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-fill-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-fill-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-focus>focus<a href=#page-focus class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to focus" title="Direct link to focus" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.focus</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-focus>locator.focus()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method fetches an element with <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-focus-option-selector>selector</a> and focuses it. If there's no element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-focus-option-selector>selector</a>, the method waits until a matching element appears in the DOM.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>

<li class="">

<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-focus-option-signal></a><a href=#page-focus-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>

<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-focus-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-focus-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-hover>hover<a href=#page-hover class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to hover" title="Direct link to hover" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.hover</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-hover>locator.hover()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method hovers over an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-hover-option-selector>selector</a> by performing the following steps:</p>
<ol>
<li class="">Find an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-hover-option-selector>selector</a>. If there is none, wait until a matching element is attached to the DOM.</li>
<li class="">Wait for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks on the matched element, unless <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-hover-option-force>force</a> option is set. If the element is detached during the checks, the whole action is retried.</li>
<li class="">Scroll the element into view if needed.</li>
<li class="">Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-mouse>page.mouse</a> to hover over the center of the element, or the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-hover-option-position>position</a>.</li>
</ol>
<p>When all steps combined have not finished during the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-hover-option-timeout>timeout</a>, this method throws a <a title=TimeoutError class="" href=/docs/api/class-timeouterror>TimeoutError</a>. Passing zero timeout disables this.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">hover</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">hover</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-selector></a><a href=#page-hover-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>force</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-force></a><a href=#page-hover-option-force class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether to bypass the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>modifiers</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Array target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Array class="">Array</a>&lt;"Alt" | "Control" | "ControlOrMeta" | "Meta" | "Shift"> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-modifiers></a><a href=#page-hover-option-modifiers class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Modifier keys to press. Ensures that only these modifiers are pressed during the operation, and then restores current modifiers back. If not specified, currently pressed modifiers are used. "ControlOrMeta" resolves to "Control" on Windows and Linux and to "Meta" on macOS.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.28</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-hover-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option has no effect.</div></div>
<p>This option has no effect.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>position</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-position></a><a href=#page-hover-option-position class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>x</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>y</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>A point to use relative to the top-left corner of element padding box. If not specified, uses some visible point of the element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scroll</code> "auto" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-scroll></a><a href=#page-hover-option-scroll class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Controls whether Playwright scrolls the element into view before performing the action. Defaults to <code>"auto"</code>, which scrolls the element into view when necessary, including scrolling nested scrollable containers. When set to <code>"none"</code>, Playwright does not scroll the element and the action fails if the element is not already in the viewport. This is useful to assert that an element is reachable by the user without additional scrolling.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-signal></a><a href=#page-hover-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-strict></a><a href=#page-hover-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-hover-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>trial</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.11</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-option-trial></a><a href=#page-hover-option-trial class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set, this method only performs the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks and skips the action. Defaults to <code>false</code>. Useful to wait until the element is ready for the action without performing it. Note that keyboard <code>modifiers</code> will be pressed regardless of <code>trial</code> to allow testing elements which are only visible when those keys are pressed.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-hover-return></a><a href=#page-hover-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
