<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-input-value>inputValue<a href=#page-input-value class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to inputValue" title="Direct link to inputValue" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.13</font>
<x-search>page.inputValue</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-input-value>locator.inputValue()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>Returns <code>input.value</code> for the selected <code>&lt;input></code> or <code>&lt;textarea></code> or <code>&lt;select></code> element.</p>
<p>Throws for non-input elements. However, if the element is inside the <code>&lt;label></code> element that has an associated <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLLabelElement/control target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">control</a>, returns the value of the control.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">inputValue</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">inputValue</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-input-value-option-selector></a><a href=#page-input-value-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-input-value-option-signal></a><a href=#page-input-value-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-input-value-option-strict></a><a href=#page-input-value-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-input-value-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-input-value-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-input-value-return></a><a href=#page-input-value-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-is-enabled>isEnabled<a href=#page-is-enabled class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to isEnabled" title="Direct link to isEnabled" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.isEnabled</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-is-enabled>locator.isEnabled()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>Returns whether the element is <a class="" href=/docs/actionability#enabled>enabled</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">isEnabled</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">isEnabled</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-is-enabled-option-selector></a><a href=#page-is-enabled-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-is-enabled-option-signal></a><a href=#page-is-enabled-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-is-enabled-option-strict></a><a href=#page-is-enabled-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-is-enabled-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-is-enabled-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-is-enabled-return></a><a href=#page-is-enabled-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-is-hidden>isHidden<a href=#page-is-hidden class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to isHidden" title="Direct link to isHidden" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.isHidden</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-is-hidden>locator.isHidden()</a> instead. <p>Returns whether the element is hidden, the opposite of <a class="" href=/docs/actionability#visible>visible</a>.  <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-is-hidden-option-selector>selector</a> that does not match any elements is considered hidden.</p>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-press>press<a href=#page-press class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to press" title="Direct link to press" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.press</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-press>locator.press()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>Focuses the element, and then uses <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-keyboard#keyboard-down>keyboard.down()</a> and <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-keyboard#keyboard-up>keyboard.up()</a>.</p>
<p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-press-option-key>key</a> can specify the intended <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/KeyboardEvent/key target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">keyboardEvent.key</a> value or a single character to generate the text for. A superset of the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-press-option-key>key</a> values can be found <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/KeyboardEvent/key/Key_Values target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">here</a>. Examples of the keys are:</p>
<p><code>F1</code> - <code>F12</code>, <code>Digit0</code>- <code>Digit9</code>, <code>KeyA</code>- <code>KeyZ</code>, <code>Backquote</code>, <code>Minus</code>, <code>Equal</code>, <code>Backslash</code>, <code>Backspace</code>, <code>Tab</code>, <code>Delete</code>, <code>Escape</code>, <code>ArrowDown</code>, <code>End</code>, <code>Enter</code>, <code>Home</code>, <code>Insert</code>, <code>PageDown</code>, <code>PageUp</code>, <code>ArrowRight</code>, <code>ArrowUp</code>, etc.</p>
<p>Following modification shortcuts are also supported: <code>Shift</code>, <code>Control</code>, <code>Alt</code>, <code>Meta</code>, <code>ShiftLeft</code>, <code>ControlOrMeta</code>. <code>ControlOrMeta</code> resolves to <code>Control</code> on Windows and Linux and to <code>Meta</code> on macOS.</p>
<p>Holding down <code>Shift</code> will type the text that corresponds to the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-press-option-key>key</a> in the upper case.</p>
<p>If <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-press-option-key>key</a> is a single character, it is case-sensitive, so the values <code>a</code> and <code>A</code> will generate different respective texts.</p>
<p>Shortcuts such as <code>key: "Control+o"</code>, <code>key: "Control++</code> or <code>key: "Control+Shift+T"</code> are supported as well. When specified with the modifier, modifier is pressed and being held while the subsequent key is being pressed.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> page </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> browser</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">newPage</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, <p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-press-option-selector></a><a href=#page-press-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>key</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-press-option-key></a><a href=#page-press-option-key class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Name of the key to press or a character to generate, such as <code>ArrowLeft</code> or <code>a</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>delay</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-press-option-delay></a><a href=#page-press-option-delay class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Time to wait between <code>keydown</code> and <code>keyup</code> in milliseconds. Defaults to 0.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-press-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-press-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option will default to <code>true</code> in the future.</div></div>
<p>Actions that initiate navigations are waiting for these navigations to happen and for pages to start loading. You can opt out of waiting via setting this flag. You would only need this option in the exceptional cases such as navigating to inaccessible pages. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-press-option-signal></a><a href=#page-press-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-press-option-strict></a><a href=#page-press-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-press-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-press-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>

<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-select-option>selectOption<a href=#page-select-option class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to selectOption" title="Direct link to selectOption" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.selectOption</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-select-option>locator.selectOption()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method waits for an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-select-option-option-selector>selector</a>, waits for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks, waits until all specified options are present in the <code>&lt;select></code> element and selects these options.</p>
<p>If the target element is not a <code>&lt;select></code> element, this method throws an error. However, if the element is inside the <code>&lt;label></code> element that has an associated <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLLabelElement/control target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">control</a>, the control will be used instead.</p>
<p>Returns the array of option values that have been successfully selected.</p>
<p>Triggers a <code>change</code> and <code>input</code> event once all the provided options have been selected.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>

<li class="">

</li>

<p>Options to select. If the <code>&lt;select></code> has the <code>multiple</code> attribute, all matching options are selected, otherwise only the first option matching one of the passed options is selected. String values are matching both values and labels. Option is considered matching if all specified properties match.</p>

</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-select-option-option-signal></a><a href=#page-select-option-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-select-option-option-strict></a><a href=#page-select-option-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-select-option-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-select-option-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Array target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Array class="">Array</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a>>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-select-option-return></a><a href=#page-select-option-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-set-checked>setChecked<a href=#page-set-checked class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to setChecked" title="Direct link to setChecked" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added in: v1.15</font>
<x-search>page.setChecked</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-set-checked>locator.setChecked()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method checks or unchecks an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-checked-option-selector>selector</a> by performing the following steps:</p>
<ol>
<li class="">Find an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-checked-option-selector>selector</a>. If there is none, wait until a matching element is attached to the DOM.</li>
<li class="">Ensure that matched element is a checkbox or a radio input. If not, this method throws.</li>
<li class="">If the element already has the right checked state, this method returns immediately.</li>
<li class="">Wait for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks on the matched element, unless <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-checked-option-force>force</a> option is set. If the element is detached during the checks, the whole action is retried.</li>
<li class="">Scroll the element into view if needed.</li>
<li class="">Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-mouse>page.mouse</a> to click in the center of the element.</li>
<li class="">Ensure that the element is now checked or unchecked. If not, this method throws.</li>
</ol>
<p>When all steps combined have not finished during the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-checked-option-timeout>timeout</a>, this method throws a <a title=TimeoutError class="" href=/docs/api/class-timeouterror>TimeoutError</a>. Passing zero timeout disables this.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">setChecked</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> checked</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">setChecked</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> checked</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>

<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>

<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>force</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-checked-option-force></a><a href=#page-set-checked-option-force class=list-anchor>#</a></p>

</li>

<p>A point to use relative to the top-left corner of element padding box. If not specified, uses some visible point of the element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scroll</code> "auto" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-checked-option-scroll></a><a href=#page-set-checked-option-scroll class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Controls whether Playwright scrolls the element into view before performing the action. Defaults to <code>"auto"</code>, which scrolls the element into view when necessary, including scrolling nested scrollable containers. When set to <code>"none"</code>, Playwright does not scroll the element and the action fails if the element is not already in the viewport. This is useful to assert that an element is reachable by the user without additional scrolling.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-checked-option-signal></a><a href=#page-set-checked-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-checked-option-strict></a><a href=#page-set-checked-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-checked-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-set-checked-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>trial</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-checked-option-trial></a><a href=#page-set-checked-option-trial class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set, this method only performs the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks and skips the action. Defaults to <code>false</code>. Useful to wait until the element is ready for the action without performing it.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-set-input-files>setInputFiles<a href=#page-set-input-files class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to setInputFiles" title="Direct link to setInputFiles" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.setInputFiles</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-set-input-files>locator.setInputFiles()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>Sets the value of the file input to these file paths or files. If some of the <code>filePaths</code> are relative paths, then they are resolved relative to the current working directory. For empty array, clears the selected files. For inputs with a <code>[webkitdirectory]</code> attribute, only a single directory path is supported.</p>
<p>This method expects <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-input-files-option-selector>selector</a> to point to an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/input target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">input element</a>. However, if the element is inside the <code>&lt;label></code> element that has an associated <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLLabelElement/control target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">control</a>, targets the control instead.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">setInputFiles</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> files</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">setInputFiles</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> files</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<p>This option has no effect.</p>

<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-input-files-option-signal></a><a href=#page-set-input-files-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-input-files-option-strict></a><a href=#page-set-input-files-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-input-files-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-set-input-files-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-set-input-files-return></a><a href=#page-set-input-files-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-tap>tap<a href=#page-tap class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to tap" title="Direct link to tap" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.tap</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-tap>locator.tap()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method taps an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-tap-option-selector>selector</a> by performing the following steps:</p>
<ol>
<li class="">Find an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-tap-option-selector>selector</a>. If there is none, wait until a matching element is attached to the DOM.</li>
<li class="">Wait for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks on the matched element, unless <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-tap-option-force>force</a> option is set. If the element is detached during the checks, the whole action is retried.</li>
<li class="">Scroll the element into view if needed.</li>
<li class="">Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-touchscreen>page.touchscreen</a> to tap the center of the element, or the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-tap-option-position>position</a>.</li>
</ol>
<p>When all steps combined have not finished during the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-tap-option-timeout>timeout</a>, this method throws a <a title=TimeoutError class="" href=/docs/api/class-timeouterror>TimeoutError</a>. Passing zero timeout disables this.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p><a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-tap>page.tap()</a> will throw if the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browser#browser-new-context-option-has-touch>hasTouch</a> option of the browser context is false.</div></div>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">tap</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">tap</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-selector></a><a href=#page-tap-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>force</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-force></a><a href=#page-tap-option-force class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Whether to bypass the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks. Defaults to <code>false</code>.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>modifiers</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Array target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Array class="">Array</a>&lt;"Alt" | "Control" | "ControlOrMeta" | "Meta" | "Shift"> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-modifiers></a><a href=#page-tap-option-modifiers class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Modifier keys to press. Ensures that only these modifiers are pressed during the operation, and then restores current modifiers back. If not specified, currently pressed modifiers are used. "ControlOrMeta" resolves to "Control" on Windows and Linux and to "Meta" on macOS.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-tap-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option has no effect.</div></div>
<p>This option has no effect.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>position</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-position></a><a href=#page-tap-option-position class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>x</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>y</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>A point to use relative to the top-left corner of element padding box. If not specified, uses some visible point of the element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scroll</code> "auto" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-scroll></a><a href=#page-tap-option-scroll class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Controls whether Playwright scrolls the element into view before performing the action. Defaults to <code>"auto"</code>, which scrolls the element into view when necessary, including scrolling nested scrollable containers. When set to <code>"none"</code>, Playwright does not scroll the element and the action fails if the element is not already in the viewport. This is useful to assert that an element is reachable by the user without additional scrolling.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-signal></a><a href=#page-tap-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-strict></a><a href=#page-tap-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-tap-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>trial</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.11</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-option-trial></a><a href=#page-tap-option-trial class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set, this method only performs the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks and skips the action. Defaults to <code>false</code>. Useful to wait until the element is ready for the action without performing it. Note that keyboard <code>modifiers</code> will be pressed regardless of <code>trial</code> to allow testing elements which are only visible when those keys are pressed.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/undefined target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=void class="">void</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-tap-return></a><a href=#page-tap-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>

<x-search>page.textContent</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-text-content>locator.textContent()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>Returns <code>element.textContent</code>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">textContent</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">textContent</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-type>type<a href=#page-type class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to type" title="Direct link to type" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.type</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>In most cases, you should use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-fill>locator.fill()</a> instead. You only need to press keys one by one if there is special keyboard handling on the page - in this case use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-press-sequentially>locator.pressSequentially()</a>.</div></div>
<p>Sends a <code>keydown</code>, <code>keypress</code>/<code>input</code>, and <code>keyup</code> event for each character in the text. <code>page.type</code> can be used to send fine-grained keyboard events. To fill values in form fields, use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-fill>page.fill()</a>.</p>
<p>To press a special key, like <code>Control</code> or <code>ArrowDown</code>, use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-keyboard#keyboard-press>keyboard.press()</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-type-option-selector></a><a href=#page-type-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to search for an element. If there are multiple elements satisfying the selector, the first will be used.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>text</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-type-option-text></a><a href=#page-type-option-text class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A text to type into a focused element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>delay</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-type-option-delay></a><a href=#page-type-option-delay class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Time to wait between key presses in milliseconds. Defaults to 0.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>noWaitAfter</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-type-option-no-wait-after></a><a href=#page-type-option-no-wait-after class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option has no effect.</div></div>

</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-type-option-signal></a><a href=#page-type-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-type-option-strict></a><a href=#page-type-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-type-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-type-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-uncheck>uncheck<a href=#page-uncheck class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to uncheck" title="Direct link to uncheck" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.uncheck</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-uncheck>locator.uncheck()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>This method unchecks an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-uncheck-option-selector>selector</a> by performing the following steps:</p>
<ol>
<li class="">Find an element matching <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-uncheck-option-selector>selector</a>. If there is none, wait until a matching element is attached to the DOM.</li>
<li class="">Ensure that matched element is a checkbox or a radio input. If not, this method throws. If the element is already unchecked, this method returns immediately.</li>
<li class="">Wait for <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks on the matched element, unless <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-uncheck-option-force>force</a> option is set. If the element is detached during the checks, the whole action is retried.</li>
<li class="">Scroll the element into view if needed.</li>
<li class="">Use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-mouse>page.mouse</a> to click in the center of the element.</li>
<li class="">Ensure that the element is now unchecked. If not, this method throws.</li>
</ol>
<p>When all steps combined have not finished during the specified <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-uncheck-option-timeout>timeout</a>, this method throws a <a title=TimeoutError class="" href=/docs/api/class-timeouterror>TimeoutError</a>. Passing zero timeout disables this.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">uncheck</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">uncheck</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token plain">selector</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">,</span><span class="token plain"> options</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>

<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This option has no effect.</div></div>

</ul>
<p>A point to use relative to the top-left corner of element padding box. If not specified, uses some visible point of the element.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>scroll</code> "auto" | "none" <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-uncheck-option-scroll></a><a href=#page-uncheck-option-scroll class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Controls whether Playwright scrolls the element into view before performing the action. Defaults to <code>"auto"</code>, which scrolls the element into view when necessary, including scrolling nested scrollable containers. When set to <code>"none"</code>, Playwright does not scroll the element and the action fails if the element is not already in the viewport. This is useful to assert that an element is reachable by the user without additional scrolling.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-uncheck-option-signal></a><a href=#page-uncheck-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>

If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">

<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-uncheck-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-uncheck-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>trial</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.11</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-uncheck-option-trial></a><a href=#page-uncheck-option-trial class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When set, this method only performs the <a class="" href=/docs/actionability>actionability</a> checks and skips the action. Defaults to <code>false</code>. Useful to wait until the element is ready for the action without performing it.</p>
