<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-navigation>waitForNavigation<a href=#page-wait-for-navigation class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForNavigation" title="Direct link to waitForNavigation" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.waitForNavigation</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Deprecated</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>This method is inherently racy, please use <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-url>page.waitForURL()</a> instead.</div></div>
<p>Waits for the main frame navigation and returns the main resource response. In case of multiple redirects, the navigation will resolve with the response of the last redirect. In case of navigation to a different anchor or navigation due to History API usage, the navigation will resolve with <code>null</code>.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>This resolves when the page navigates to a new URL or reloads. It is useful for when you run code which will indirectly cause the page to navigate. e.g. The click target has an <code>onclick</code> handler that triggers navigation from a <code>setTimeout</code>. Consider this example:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// Start waiting for navigation before clicking. Note no await.</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword" style=color:#C586C0>const</span><span class="token plain"> navigationPromise </span><span class="token operator" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">=</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">waitForNavigation</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">getByText</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token string" style="color:rgb(206, 145, 120)">'Navigate after timeout'</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">click</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> navigationPromise</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Usage of the <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/History_API target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">History API</a> to change the URL is considered a navigation.</div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-navigation-option-signal></a><a href=#page-wait-for-navigation-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the waiting using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the waiting will be aborted and the operation will throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-navigation-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-wait-for-navigation-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum operation time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>navigationTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-navigation-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a>, <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-navigation-timeout>page.setDefaultNavigationTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>url</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/RegExp target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=RegExp class="">RegExp</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/URLPattern target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URLPattern class="">URLPattern</a> | <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Function target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Function class="">function</a>(<a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a>):<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-navigation-option-url></a><a href=#page-wait-for-navigation-option-url class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A glob pattern, regex pattern, URL pattern, or predicate receiving <a href=https://nodejs.org/api/url.html target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=URL class="">URL</a> to match while waiting for the navigation. Note that if the parameter is a string without wildcard characters, the method will wait for navigation to URL that is exactly equal to the string.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>waitUntil</code> "load" | "domcontentloaded" | "networkidle" | "commit" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-navigation-option-wait-until></a><a href=#page-wait-for-navigation-option-wait-until class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When to consider operation succeeded, defaults to <code>load</code>. Events can be either:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'domcontentloaded'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>DOMContentLoaded</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'load'</code> - consider operation to be finished when the <code>load</code> event is fired.</li>
<li class=""><code>'networkidle'</code> - <strong>DISCOURAGED</strong> consider operation to be finished when there are no network connections for at least <code>500</code> ms. Don't use this method for testing, rely on web assertions to assess readiness instead.</li>
<li class=""><code>'commit'</code> - consider operation to be finished when network response is received and the document started loading.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | <a title=Response class="" href=/docs/api/class-response>Response</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-navigation-return></a><a href=#page-wait-for-navigation-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-selector>waitForSelector<a href=#page-wait-for-selector class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForSelector" title="Direct link to waitForSelector" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.waitForSelector</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Use web assertions that assert visibility or a locator-based <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator#locator-wait-for>locator.waitFor()</a> instead. Read more about <a class="" href=/docs/locators>locators</a>.</div></div>
<p>Returns when element specified by selector satisfies <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-selector-option-state>state</a> option. Returns <code>null</code> if waiting for <code>hidden</code> or <code>detached</code>.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_xJq3 alert alert--secondary"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"/></svg></span>note</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Playwright automatically waits for element to be ready before performing an action. Using <a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a> objects and web-first assertions makes the code wait-for-selector-free.</div></div>
<p>Wait for the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-selector-option-selector>selector</a> to satisfy <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-selector-option-state>state</a> option (either appear/disappear from dom, or become visible/hidden). If at the moment of calling the method <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-selector-option-selector>selector</a> already satisfies the condition, the method will return immediately. If the selector doesn't satisfy the condition for the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-selector-option-timeout>timeout</a> milliseconds, the function will throw.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<p>This method works across navigations:</p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code <p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>selector</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#String_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=string class="">string</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-selector-option-selector></a><a href=#page-wait-for-selector-option-selector class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A selector to query for.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>options</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Object target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Object class="">Object</a> <em>(optional)</em></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>signal</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=AbortSignal class="">AbortSignal</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.62</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-selector-option-signal></a><a href=#page-wait-for-selector-option-signal class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Allows to cancel the operation using an <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/AbortSignal target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" class=""><code>AbortSignal</code></a>. If the signal is aborted, the operation will be aborted and throw an error. Note that providing a signal does not disable the default timeout, which can be changed using <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a>; pass <code>timeout: 0</code> to disable the timeout entirely.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>state</code> "attached" | "detached" | "visible" | "hidden" <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-selector-option-state></a><a href=#page-wait-for-selector-option-state class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Defaults to <code>'visible'</code>. Can be either:</p>
<ul>
<li class=""><code>'attached'</code> - wait for element to be present in DOM.</li>
<li class=""><code>'detached'</code> - wait for element to not be present in DOM.</li>
<li class=""><code>'visible'</code> - wait for element to have non-empty bounding box and no <code>visibility:hidden</code>. Note that element without any content or with <code>display:none</code> has an empty bounding box and is not considered visible.</li>
<li class=""><code>'hidden'</code> - wait for element to be either detached from DOM, or have an empty bounding box or <code>visibility:hidden</code>. This is opposite to the <code>'visible'</code> option.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>strict</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Boolean_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Boolean class="">boolean</a> <em>(optional)</em> <font size=2>Added in: v1.14</font><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-selector-option-strict></a><a href=#page-wait-for-selector-option-strict class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>When true, the call requires selector to resolve to a single element. If given selector resolves to more than one element, the call throws an exception.</p>
</li>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a> <em>(optional)</em><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-selector-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-wait-for-selector-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>Maximum time in milliseconds. Defaults to <code>0</code> - no timeout. The default value can be changed via <code>actionTimeout</code> option in the config, or by using the <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-browsercontext#browser-context-set-default-timeout>browserContext.setDefaultTimeout()</a> or <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-set-default-timeout>page.setDefaultTimeout()</a> methods.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Returns</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class=""><a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Promise class="">Promise</a>&lt;<a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/null target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=null class="">null</a> | <a title=ElementHandle class="" href=/docs/api/class-elementhandle>ElementHandle</a>><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-selector-return></a><a href=#page-wait-for-selector-return class=list-anchor>#</a></li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Vzrq" id=page-wait-for-timeout>waitForTimeout<a href=#page-wait-for-timeout class=hash-link aria-label="Direct link to waitForTimeout" title="Direct link to waitForTimeout" translate=no>​</a></h3>
<font size=2 style=position:relative;top:-20px>Added before v1.9</font>
<x-search>page.waitForTimeout</x-search>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_xJq3 alert alert--warning"><div class=admonitionHeading_Gvgb><span class=admonitionIcon_Rf37><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule=evenodd d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"/></svg></span>Discouraged</div><div class=admonitionContent_BuS1><p>Never wait for timeout in production. Tests that wait for time are inherently flaky. Use <a title=Locator class="" href=/docs/api/class-locator>Locator</a> actions and web assertions that wait automatically.</div></div>
<p>Waits for the given <a class="" href=/docs/api/class-page#page-wait-for-timeout-option-timeout>timeout</a> in milliseconds.</p>
<p>Note that <code>page.waitForTimeout()</code> should only be used for debugging. Tests using the timer in production are going to be flaky. Use signals such as network events, selectors becoming visible and others instead.</p>
<p><strong>Usage</strong></p>
<div class="language-js codeBlockContainer_Ckt0 theme-code-block" style=--prism-color:#D4D4D4;--prism-background-color:#212121><div class=codeBlockContent_QJqH><pre tabindex=0 class="prism-code language-js codeBlock_bY9V thin-scrollbar" style=color:#D4D4D4;background-color:#212121><code class=codeBlockLines_e6Vv><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token comment" style="color:rgb(106, 153, 85)">// wait for 1 second</span><span class="token plain"></span><br/></div><div class=token-line style=color:#D4D4D4><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword control-flow" style=color:#C586C0>await</span><span class="token plain"> page</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">.</span><span class="token method function property-access" style="color:rgb(220, 220, 170)">waitForTimeout</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">(</span><span class="token number" style=color:#B5CEA8>1000</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">)</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color:rgb(212, 212, 212)">;</span><br/></div></code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Arguments</strong></p>
<ul>
<li class="">
<p><code>timeout</code> <a href=https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Data_structures#Number_type target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer" title=Number class="">number</a><a aria-hidden=true tabindex=-1 class=list-anchor-link id=page-wait-for-timeout-option-timeout></a><a href=#page-wait-for-timeout-option-timeout class=list-anchor>#</a></p>
<p>A timeout to wait for</p>
</li>
</ul>
